# cyrene — Personal Memories

> Verbatim dialogue from the canon databank where this Heir appears, copied (never modified in the databank) to serve as personal memory. Each part shows the source file and the in-story context (chapter / mission / scene), and is bounded to a coherent dialogue moment.

---

### Part 3 — `databank/missions/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`

**Context:** Complete Dialogue > Mission 5 — Night Veil, Shroud the Silent Past > Assist the Stellaron Hunters


**Oronyx:** I've finally found it... Precious memory. At long last... I've caught THEIR gaze.

**Oronyx:** He/She will make the memories of this world complete once again... He/She will find... Mother...

> **Mem:** Name? Mine? Don't remember.
> **Mem:** Mem... Address. Temporarily.

> **Mem:** Mother? Who? Don't remember.
> *(Trailblazer)* Is your name "Mem"?
> **Mem:** Name? Mine? Don't remember.
> **Mem:** Mem... Address. For now.
> **Mem:** Travel, like. With you, together?
> **Mem:** Memory... collected. Piece together, complete.
> *(Trailblazer)* Nikador's weakness...
> *(Trailblazer)* ...Can you help us find it?
> **Mem:** Ador... Nik? Titan?
> **Mem:** Need, memories. Help... I can.

**Oronyx:** They... are your companions?
> *(Trailblazer)* Yes, they are my companions.
> *(Trailblazer)* I also can't say for sure...
> *(Trailblazer)* No, they are enemies.

### Part 4 — `databank/missions/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`

**Context:** Complete Dialogue > Mission 5 — Night Veil, Shroud the Silent Past > Assist the Stellaron Hunters


**Silver Wolf:** You're finally here, (Trailblazer).
**Sam:** I knew you wouldn't miss this, (Trailblazer).
**Blade:** The script says, "Leave no survivors." ...(Trailblazer).
**Kafka:** I'm glad to have you here, (Trailblazer).
**Kafka:** This path is anything but smooth... it's marked by flames, scars, malice, and bloodshed. But I guarantee, as long as you stand with the Stellaron Hunters, you will never know the pain of betrayal.
**Kafka:** (Trailblazer)... Come, join us on stage. In the "script," we are the most brilliant actors on the cosmic stage, and the focus will always be on you.
**Kafka:** All the bloodshed and cries beyond the screen are merely there to serve as the backdrop for the main characters. Together, we will dance against the current, until the universe meets the Finality.
**Sam:** Let's dance, (Trailblazer).
**Silver Wolf:** Let's dance, (Trailblazer).
**Blade:** Let's dance, (Trailblazer).
**Kafka:** Let's dance, (Trailblazer)!
*(Begin battle)*
**Silver Wolf:** Ugh, why do I have to be the standby support again? So boring...
**Mem:** Battle... Companions, together!
*(Battle lines)*
**Silver Wolf:** Hack successful. Condition recovered!
**Blade:** It's time to clean up...
**Silver Wolf:** Take this. System crash!
**Blade:** Savor it? You're unworthy!
**Kafka:** Leave no survivors... Right?
**Silver Wolf:** It's all about that adrenaline rush!
**Kafka:** The melody of slaughter, how beautiful.
**(Trailblazer):** We are... companions.
**Silver Wolf:** Stand still so I can beat you!
**(Trailblazer):** In this script... I am the main character!
**Sam:** (Trailblazer), let's dance together.
**Silver Wolf:** Blaming me for cheating? Hmph, ban my account if you dare!
**Sam:** Together, we shall... set the seas ablaze.

### Part 6 — `databank/missions/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`

**Context:** Complete Dialogue > Mission 5 — Night Veil, Shroud the Silent Past > Share Your "Non-Existent Memory" with Dan Heng


> *(Trailblazer)* Fuli gazed at me.
> **Dan Heng:** Fuli? Just now?
> **Phainon:** Fuli... What's that?
> **Dan Heng:** Remember the Aeons that I spoke to you about on the way to Okhema? Fuli is one of them and presides over the path of Remembrance.
> **Phainon:** Ahh... A god from the worlds beyond the sky. A miracle that is hard to see with the naked eye... Right?
> **Phainon:** To earn the gaze of an Aeon, does that mean...
> *(Trailblazer)* Mem says that they can help us.
> *(Trailblazer)* It's time for us to deal with Nikador.
> **Mem:** Titan, past... Foggy.
> **Mem:** **Collect. Past... Reappear.**
> *(Trailblazer)* We must collect the memories of Kremnos.
> *(Trailblazer)* We have to recreate the past.
> **Phainon:** You can understand what they are saying?
> **Phainon:** Collect the memories and recreate the past... How can we do that? We don't have much time left.
> **Mem:** Anxious, no need! I will. Teach you.
> *(Trailblazer)* Mem will tell us what we have to do.
> *(Trailblazer)* Let's follow them. We don't have any better ideas right now.
> **Dan Heng:** If they truly are a creation of Fuli, or have some connection to that Aeon, I'm willing to believe they can wield a power greater than Oronyx's Miracle.
> **Dan Heng:** Let's go, we need to follow them.

### Part 7 — `databank/missions/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`

**Context:** Complete Dialogue > Mission 6 — Kremnos, Cleanse Thy Rusted Blood (I) > Collect the Memory Fragments Related to Castrum Kremnos (0/3)


*(Tribbie took everyone back to Okhema. You decided to form two teams: Phainon and Dan Heng would go to Castrum Kremnos to help Mydei, while you and Castorice would use Mem's power to find Nikador's weakness.)*
**Phainon:** Thank goodness we no longer have to ride the dromas into the city. Thanks for your hard work, Tribbie.
**Tribbie:** No need for formalities at a time like this. I trust that you have found a solution?
**Phainon:** Simply put, Oronyx refused to help us uncover the past of Castrum Kremnos. But for some reason, they showed interest toward (Trailblazer).
**Phainon:** They... through some unknown means... gifted (Trailblazer) this pet—
**Mem:** Pet. Mem — isn't!
**Phainon:** According to Dan Heng, this creature is related to a god from beyond the sky. Perhaps they can help us solve the problem.
**Tribbie:** A god from beyond the sky...
**Phainon:** Tribbie, let's split up here. I'll make my way to Kremnos immediately to lend my support to Mydei.
**Phainon:** As for the mission of finding out Nikador's weakness... Can I leave it to you, (Trailblazer)? I ask this sincerely from the bottom of my heart.
> *(Trailblazer)* Just leave it to me and Mem.
> *(Trailblazer)* Be careful, you're not indestructible.
> **Phainon:** Don't worry about me, surviving the impossible is my specialty.
> **Phainon:** Mydei and I will be waiting for you on the battlefield.
**Dan Heng:** Let me come too, Phainon. I'd rather be on the front lines, helping ease the burden.
**Phainon:** ...Thank you. I don't have any reason to refuse.
**Phainon:** Then this is where we part ways — Dan Heng and I will head to Castrum Kremnos immediately, while (Trailblazer) works on uncovering the secret behind the barbaric god's indestructible body. As for miss Castorice...
**Castorice:** I'll stay with (Trailblazer) to ensure his/her safety.
**Phainon:** I'll leave it to you. Tribbie... Could I trouble you once again?
**Tribbie:** Of course, just leave the Century Gate to us.
**Castorice:** We're the only ones left... Where should we go to find the lost memories?
**Mem:** Memories... Smell, intense.
**Mem:** Tracking, found. Past... Recreate.
> *(Trailblazer)* Let's just believe in Mem's sense of smell.
> **Mem:** Mem, believe. Follow, friends!
> *(Trailblazer)* So, you're actually a dog?
> **Mem:** Pet, not!
> **Mem:** Mem, believe. Follow, friends!

### Part 8 — `databank/missions/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`

**Context:** Complete Dialogue > Mission 6 — Kremnos, Cleanse Thy Rusted Blood (I) > Collect the Memory Fragments Related to Castrum Kremnos (0/3)


*(Approach Bard Lipos)*
**Mem:** Here. Here!
**Mem:** Memories, ballads... Collect!
*(You talked to the Bard Lipos, who sang you a poem about Nikador's madness. Mem collected memory fragments about Castrum Kremnos from the poem.)*
*(Talk to Bard Lipos)*
**Bard Lipos:** Oh, elegant lady! Are you here to listen to Lipos' song?
**Bard Lipos:** What are you in the mood for? The love stories of the ancient Chrysos Heirs? The turbulent era of the Chrysos War? The rise and fall of the Seaside States? Lipos has it all!
**Castorice:** I wonder if there is a ballad about Nikador's madness as well as the mystery of Castrum Kremnos vanishing into the fog.
**Bard Lipos:** Ma'am, you have come to the right place! There is such a ballad within my collection that has yet to grace the ears of others.
**Bard Lipos:** Allow me to sing it for you—
**Bard Lipos:** "The war of a thousand years has drawn to its close. And now the world descends into a long night of chaos. O valiant Titan, tell me, how did your golden heart shatter into a thousand fragments?"
**Bard Lipos:** "O god, have you grown weary of endless strife? Then how do you justify your ruthless tyranny? And how shall you cast off the darkness that so deeply shrouds your divinity?"
**Bard Lipos:** "O god, have you forgotten the glory of old? Then how will you defend your dignity? And where shall your faithful subjects find their home?"
**Bard Lipos:** "O mighty Nikador, the truth is clear to us all. Your steadfast divinity is not vanquished. You are shrouded momentarily by that encroaching black tide."
**Bard Lipos:** "But you never faltered, nor did you flee! Your golden warrior spirit, brave and bold, will conquer the black tide, as foretold. To the throne of Kremnos, you'll return once more!"
**Bard Lipos:** "Our god, our king, to aid your timeless struggle, we have prepared a grand ritual—"
**Bard Lipos:** "Please grant us the honor to touch your soul with our humble hands, and present to you this sacred Sword Vessel... It shall consecrate your eternal life and enshrine your everlasting glory."
**Mem:** There, done. Memory, collected.
**Castorice:** Thank you, esteemed bard with an enchanting voice. Your ballads will endure through the ages, serving as a bulwark for Okhema.
*(Obtain Bard's Memory Fragment)*

### Part 14 — `databank/missions/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`

**Context:** Complete Dialogue > Mission 6 — Kremnos, Cleanse Thy Rusted Blood (I) > Collect the Memory Fragments Related to Castrum Kremnos


> *(Trailblazer)* You almost got me killed!
> **Damionis:** My apologies, that was entirely my fault. I was determined to prove that a world beyond the sky exists...
> **Damionis:** Fortunately, Lady Aglaea is quite lenient. She let me go after I made a vow.
> *(Trailblazer)* You're out already?
> **Damionis:** Lady Aglaea is quite lenient. She let me go after I made a vow.
> **Damionis:** Rest assured, I'm not foolish enough to make the same mistake twice. Breaking a vow with Lady Aglaea would be like... digging my own grave.
> *(Trailblazer)* Do you have any memories related to Castrum Kremnos?
> **Damionis:** Castrum Kremnos? For us unarmed civilians, Nikador's territory is off-limits. Even in the past...
> **Damionis:** Oh, n–no... Wait, I just remembered! I did visit Castrum Kremnos once. Let me find it for you...
> **Damionis:** Y–Yes, yes, this is the one! I can't believe I still have it...
**Castorice:** Could you tell us the story behind it?
**Damionis:** During the final days of the Chrysos War, the final festival took place in Castrum Kremnos. However, due to the tense situation, few outsiders were willing to compete.
**Damionis:** I gathered the courage to go there, but I found the atmosphere of the festival to be... quite strange. It felt more like the Kremnoans were preparing for a mysterious ceremony.
**Damionis:** Sensing something was off, I snapped a photo outside the city and then left... And what followed was something completely unexpected.
**Castorice:** Would you mind giving us the photo? We're... trying to piece together clues from that time.
**Damionis:** Sure, take it! Think of it as my way of making it up to you.
**Mem:** Done. Memory, collected.
*(Obtain Damionis' Memory Fragment)*

### Part 21 — `databank/missions/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`

**Context:** Complete Dialogue > Mission 8 — Kremnos, Cleanse Thy Rusted Blood (II) > Return to the Private Bath Chamber and Rest


*(Returning to the bath chamber, Mem appeared by your side. It was now able to form full sentences, saying that as long as you continued collecting the scattered memories, good things would follow. And so, with Mem beside you, you drifted into peaceful sleep.)*
*(Approach the marked location)*
*(narrative)* Exhaustion envelops you... At this moment, no god of Amphoreus could pull your gaze away from this lounge chair.
**Mem:** Aww, are you sleepy? I can see your eyelids growing heavy~
**Mem:** Go to sleep then, if you're tired. Snoozy snooze... But isn't it always daytime in this city? How do you fall asleep like this...?
*(narrative)* Wait... Something seems off...
*(narrative)* ...Did you notice?
**Mem:** Eep! I'll get embarrassed if you keep staring at me like that.
**Mem:** Are there... knots in my fur? Is it my ears? No, they seem fine. Look, I can even flap them!
> *(Trailblazer)* ...You can say more than single words now!?
> *(Trailblazer)* Wow, so you can speak in full sentences!
> **Mem:** ...Hmm? You're right! I am speaking in full sentences now!
> **Mem:** Wowie! It feels amazing being able to speak fluently! But... why do I speak in such a proper, un-cutesy manner now? I mean, referring to myself in standard first person and all.
> *(Trailblazer)* Maybe it gives you more personality.
> **Mem:** Personality...? Hmph, you're always saying things I don't understand!
> *(Trailblazer)* Why aren't your words enunciated?
> **Mem:** Right!? You noticed too? Hmph, no fair!
> *(Trailblazer)* Ew, phooey!
> **Mem:** I didn't ask to be like this! No judging!
> **Mem:** Hmph, never mind, since I'm the forgiving type. Besides, now that I'm a bit more used to it... I don't sound as bad?
> *(Trailblazer)* Wait, who are you really?
> *(Trailblazer)* So, can you tell me about yourself now?
> **Mem:** Huh... I don't remember a thing about myself. Ugh, my head hurts.
> **Mem:** I have a gut feeling, though! As long as we continue to collect the fragments of memories scattered across Amphoreus, we'll be rewarded with something really, really good.
> **Mem:** How should I put it... Bah, anyway! I just know that this world is very important to me!
> *(Trailblazer)* Sorry to burst your bubble, but I'm the protagonist here.
> **Mem:** Then I'll be the protagonist's bestest sidekick, heehee! So give me more screen time, alright?
> *(Trailblazer)* So, once again, it's all down to one gal/guy and her/his dog.
> **Mem:** I'm not a dog! Or a pet! I am...
> **Mem:** ...Uh, what am I? A—Anyhow, I'm an important character for sure!
> *(Trailblazer)* We're an ensemble cast.
> **Mem:** Then I'll be the protagonist's bestest sidekick, heehee! So give me more screen time, alright?
> **Mem:** Hnngh... I think I'm getting sleepy. The light feels so nice and warm. Even my fur's toasty warm. I can fall asleep any...
> *(Trailblazer)* I'll take a break too...
> **Mem:** Zzz... Zzz...

### Part 22 — `databank/missions/chapter-01-heroic-saga.md`

**Context:** Complete Dialogue > Mission 9 — A Witch's Scientific Repose > Defeat the Out-of-Control Herta Puppets / Head Deeper to Investigate the Puppet's Signal


*(After the battle)*
**Fourth Mirror:** Phew, that was close... I almost shattered...
**Herta:** Don't be melodramatic. Working even a single brain cell of mine to take out foes like her is an outright waste of my smarts.
**Fourth Mirror:** I take it we've taken care of the problems in all three zones? Does that mean...?
**Herta:** Not yet. There's something else we need to deal with.
**Herta:** It seems we've had a little tagalong all this while...
**Fourth Mirror:** You've got quite the sharp eye, Madam Herta. Would you like us to help?
**Herta:** Sure, go ahead and summon the whole team, Fourth Mirror.
**Herta:** Considering the gravity of our endeavor, we can't allow a little thief to spoil the show.
**Herta:** Hmph, parasite of the Remembrance. Do you truly think your atrocious disguise can outwit the keen sight of a genius like myself?
**Herta:** Once you're reflected in my mirrors...
**Herta:** ...you can forget about escaping.
**Memosnatcher:** Oh dear, oh dear. This is bad...
**Memosnatcher:** Right, stay alive and again I can strive... Retreat into the Memory Zone for now, I can always regroup and try again!
**Herta:** Someone hasn't been paying attention.
**Herta:** Didn't hear me, did you? With those petty tricks of yours... do you actually think you can slip away right under my nose?
**Memosnatcher:** Herta... Madam Herta, wait! Wh—What do you want with me...?
**Memosnatcher:** I... I'll spill it all! The Garden's secrets and what they're after... everything! P—Please just spare me...
**Herta:** Well, is that all you've got to say while your life is at stake? That's hardly worth anything.
**Herta:** Need a little refresher? I am Herta, Genius Society #83, the one to unravel the mystery behind the imaginary leakage phenomenon, and keyholder of the Imaginary Implosion Pulse... Do you honestly believe I'd seek any counsel from you?
**Memosnatcher:** I... I...
**Herta:** Hush now, keep quiet. This is my moment to shine, so just listen.
**Herta:** You crept in here to steal **Nous' memories**, didn't you?
**Herta:** And messing with my precious Audience System is just a ruse to lure me here so I can lead you to THEM...
**Herta:** What a shame. You're only a step away from completing the mission assigned to you...
**Memosnatcher:** Mmph...! Nngh...!
**Herta:** ...As if. Truth is, you never stood a chance from the get-go, teehee!
**Herta:** Think I only have Fourth Mirror following me around? Then you've let your guard down, because her siblings have long been watching your every move. Now, let me think... What kind of punishment should I subject a brazen thief like you to?
**Herta:** Ooh, I know! You Garden folk have a fondness for mirrors, huh... How about letting you enjoy some quality time with your favorite object — a mirror?
**Herta:** As for the length of your punishment... Let's keep it mildly punitive. **How about a hundredth of a lifetime?** That should be enough for some serious self-reflection.
**Herta:** Aww, cheer up. You can still do your part for this world while trapped in a mirror. For all you know, your weak Path Energy could just help me get Droidhead looking this way.
**Herta:** Ah, I should make myself clear. When I said **a hundredth of a lifetime**...
**Herta:** ...I meant my lifetime.
**Herta:** Ahh, at long last... We can finally be at ease now that we've nipped those problems in the bud.
**Fourth Mirror:** Omnomnom... Mmm, this is yummy...
**Herta:** Cut that out! Those weird noises make me seem like I've got a few screws loose. Put that little thief to sleep for now. We can decide her fate later.
**Fourth Mirror:** Oh, sweet and venerable Madam Herta, when did your heart turn this tender?
**Herta:** The Audience System has been rebooted. This is finally it...
**Herta:** After all that I've done to reach you, Droidhead... You'd better not snub me.

### Part 66 — `databank/missions/chapter-03-through-petals.md`

**Context:** Complete Dialogue > Speak to Castorice and Aglaea


> *(Trailblazer)* WE are ready, Mem and me.
> **Aglaea:** Mmm, it seems that this sacred beast is just as indispensable, given their intricate ties to the Titan.
> *(Trailblazer)* I'm still on the fence...
> **Aglaea:** I understand. It was the sacred beast's idea, after all. However, it warrants careful consideration.
**Mem:** Hehe, thanks to you, I've evolved from a little dog into a sacred beast!
**Aglaea:** Surrendering the Coreflame is a Chrysos Heir's duty. Your selfless assistance is deeply appreciated. However, I urge you to carefully consider the risks of accepting the trial: Becoming a demigod means forsaking your humanity. You will inherit Oronyx's divinity and bear the Time of Amphoreus, all on your own. From that point onward, your fate will be forever intertwined with ours, never to be separated again — until the end of Genesis.
> *(Trailblazer)* Are you trying to talk me out of it?
> **Aglaea:** On the contrary, you are the only one who qualifies to attain divinity. Yet, for that very reason, I do not wish to pressure you.
>> *Is there a plan B?*
> **Aglaea:** It's exceedingly rare to find a capable Chrysos Heir. If this path proves unattainable, I am willing to lower my expectations. There are still many priests of Janusopolis and their descendants in the city. If I'm left with no other option, I'll try to find someone worthy among them.
>> *Seems like a shot in the dark.*
> **Mem:** Exactly! We can't have the civilians bear the risks, can we?
>> *If I do not descend into hell, who will?*
> **Mem:** Don't worry! With me by your side, even if things go south, you'll be heading straight to heaven!
>> *Alright, that's it. I am out—*
> **Mem:** Don't give up now, (Trailblazer)! We've come so far, and everyone is counting on you to be the hero! As for the trial, you have nothing to fear... The Time Titan may be willful at times, but they never caused us any harm. Myth has it that Oronyx opposes mortals, but to me, they just seem a bit timid... like a child.
**Aglaea:** It looks like you know that Titan rather well.
**Mem:** Oddly enough, I don't remember ever meeting them, but the moment I saw (Trailblazer), Oronyx's memories were already in my head.
**Aglaea:** ...Memories that exist outside of your own experiences?
**Castorice:** I can't explain why, but I can vouch for Mem's words. When I was in the Abyss, I heard the Titan's groans — full of pain and grievances, yet without any ill will.
> *(Trailblazer)* I trust you all to make the right call.
> *(Trailblazer)* Do I even have any say in this?
> **Mem:** Please, trust me on this. At the very least... can you speak with them in person? I get the feeling that if we don't meet Oronyx, we'll be missing out on something huge!
**Aglaea:** Since you've made your decision, go back to the bath chamber and get some rest. Trinnon will call for you when the ceremony is ready. It is difficult for me as the guide to absolve myself from blame for things to have progressed to this point. Time and again, you have risen to the occasion for Okhema. Merely rewarding you with some Honeydew is no longer able to express the extent of our gratitude. When the dust settles, I hope that we'll have the chance to have a long chat regarding your return journey and the curse of the sky... It's about time that we lift the taboo and discuss things openly.

---

### Part 67 — `databank/missions/chapter-03-through-petals.md`

**Context:** Complete Dialogue > Speak to Castorice


**Castorice:** No, it's just an ordinary book. It's definitely not some kind of unique copy. It just contains an allegory: In a faraway land, in a city known as Styxia, a dragon corrupted by the black tide descended one day, swooping upon the city like Death itself. It swallowed the princess of the city whole before it was suppressed. After taking down the evil dragon, the warriors discovered that the princess' life had already met its end. Overwhelmed by grief, the queen hired an alchemist to resurrect the princess using the dragon's flesh and blood. However, having lost memories of her previous life, the resurrected princess turned into an evil dragon herself and wreaked havoc on the city... As a result, the prosperous Styxia met its demise.
> *(Trailblazer)* What a dark story...
> **Mem:** It mustn't have been easy for Cas to narrate this story so calmly...
> *(Trailblazer)* What a touching story...
> **Mem:** Huh? How... How was it touching...?
> **Castorice:** Hm, I actually think so, too.
> **Mem:** What!? That was the correct option to increase friendship level?
**Castorice:** The author of this book is Archepia, a wandering bard who traveled to many cities. Among the things she left behind are poems, dictionaries, and many tragicomedy scripts. She even clearly underlined and took notes in books that covered myths with her elegant handwriting. While flipping through these books, I felt like I was afforded a glimpse into the life of Archepia... It felt just like I was traveling with someone that I'd never met before. It was as if she had yet to pass on and was still alive.

**Marketplace Merchant:** I'm not selling my fish to... living people. You lot are too... shameless. Buying fish without paying... If I ever see her again, I will... I will...

**Marketplace Merchant:** Titan... Rejects... You... Me...

*(Talk to the Centurion)*
**Centurion:** Ah, by the looks of it... you must be the foreign warriors that the queen had mentioned, right?
**Centurion:** Since the current campaign requires the utmost care, I wasn't able to make time to welcome all of you. Please, forgive me.

**Centurion:** A priestess, a warrior, and a, um... a divine beast... You look identical to the blessed saviors that the queen described!

> **Centurion:** As for the rest of the scouts... They've completely lost their souls and are decaying by the day.
> **Centurion:** While there is still dissension within the city, the scholars agree that this calamity was so unprecedented that it needed to be given a new designation.
> **Castorice:** An unprecedented calamity? Could it be... "Death"?

> **Centurion:** He said that he witnessed the demonic specter of an evil dragon at that treacherous land. The apparition opened its giant maw and devoured everything in sight, and thus brought Styxia that thing called "Death."
> **Castorice:** Styxia... an evil dragon that brings Death...
> **Mem:** It all sounds rather familiar... Wait, wasn't this part of that fable?
> **Castorice:** Sir, could I... ask how the queen and princess are doing?
> **Centurion:** The queen?
> **Centurion:** — Ah, by the looks of it... you must be the foreign warriors that the queen had mentioned, right?
> *(Trailblazer)* Echo?
> *(Trailblazer)* I think he asked that already.
> *(Trailblazer)* Is he set to loop on repeat?
> **Mem:** It's the same lines... Looks like he's still trapped in the moments before his death.
> **Castorice:** ...Leave it to me.
> **Castorice:** Sir, the war ended long ago. Styxia... no longer exists.
> **Centurion:** Styxia... is no more?
> **Centurion:** Th—This cannot stand! Newcomers, hurry up and go take your positions!
> **Mem:** Looks like it's difficult for this gentleman to accept reality.
> **Castorice:** ...
> **Castorice:** Sir, I am the Maiden of War sent here by the queen herself. If you accept my blessing, then today's battle will be smooth sailing.
> **Centurion:** Maiden of War? I've never heard of this... There's little time to waste. Please don't take too long.
> **Castorice:** Don't worry. I'm typically... unwilling to prolong such a moment.
> **Centurion:** Is it over...? Have we... won?
> **Castorice:** ...
> **Mem:** Cas... Are you all right?
> **Castorice:** I—I'm fine. I just didn't expect that I would still have to "embalm" others even in the realm of the dead...
> **Castorice:** Is this... a remnant of his soul?

### Part 93 — `databank/missions/chapter-03-through-petals.md`

**Context:** Complete Dialogue > Sub-Mission 8: Ferryman, Ferry Me Across the Stream of Souls > Continue Exploring Dragonbone City and Speak with the Spirit of the Court Scholar


*(Listen to the Aidonian Spirit, optional)*
**Aidonian Spirit of the Dead:** Ah... Why isn't there a god yelling in the distance...
**Aidonian Spirit of the Dead:** "Indeed, my downtrodden fellow... Your sorrow and joy are but means for me to amuse myself."
**Aidonian Spirit of the Dead:** I will at least derive some comfort... If all the tears that I had shed were determined by some mightier being above.
**Castorice:** It's... you people...
*(Go through the door)*
**Mem:** This place... must have been a beautiful courtyard in the past.
*(Approach the Court Scholar)*
**Court Scholar:** Please excuse me. I am but a scholar from a distant land who had the misfortune of falling into the River of Souls and turned into a member of the dead...
**Mem:** Huh? This one... seems to know that she's already dead.
**Court Scholar:** Oh, I was merely speculating. But it seems I was right.
**Court Scholar:** And you... You... I recognize you. You're the Holy Maiden of "Death" from Aidonia, right?
**Castorice:** I'm sorry... I cannot bring you back to life. However, should you desire it, I can end your prolonged limbo.
**Court Scholar:** Hah, I'm well aware of that. Even if you were able to revive me, I would never choose that option.
**Court Scholar:** That's because I didn't fall into the River of Souls by accident. Instead, I leaped into it to seek my death.
**Castorice:** ...Why?
**Court Scholar:** If you could see the life I've led, then you would not reproach me so. My life... was far too miserable.
**Court Scholar:** But I did not expect that things stay the same even in death! What a joke! In escaping my mortal life, I've entered a millennium of pain instead.
**Court Scholar:** Cerces above! What cowards humans are, for we fear both life and death simultaneously?
**Castorice:** I'm sorry... I'm also powerless to answer that question.
**Court Scholar:** That's alright, Holy Maiden. At least you are able to respond to me. Please... grant me "Death."
**Court Scholar:** Cerces above... Does this count as thinking till the moment I die?
> *(Trailblazer)* She seems... free.
> *(Trailblazer)* I can't imagine what it'd feel like to roam around this void for centuries...
> **Castorice:** I have seen too many farewells... Her distress is quite commonplace. There are those who will end their lives over what seems to be the smallest of worries.
> **Castorice:** For some people... This power that I possess is a "blessing" rather than a curse.
> **Castorice:** ...But I never managed to convince myself of that.
> **Mem:** Cas... Are you still trying to create a world without "Death"?
> **Castorice:** Yes. Because... When placed under time's vast scales, there is no worry in this world that cannot be solved.
> *(Trailblazer)* You should chat with Dan Heng about this.
> *(Trailblazer)* I can't agree with you on this.
> **Castorice:** This is a wish that I've held for almost a millennium. My mind might have changed once upon a time if people managed to strike up a friendship with me...
> **Castorice:** But I've never been more firm in my conviction, now that I've learned about your near-death state — It is my duty to wipe "Death" from the face of this world.

### Part 97 — `databank/missions/chapter-03-through-petals.md`

**Context:** Complete Dialogue > Sub-Mission 8: Ferryman, Ferry Me Across the Stream of Souls > Check the Souls Remains


**Castorice:** This is his...
*(Listen to the souls remains)*
**"Amunet":** Then, you granted him death?
**"Amunet":** That isn't like you... You would have refused even if I commanded you to do so.
**"Castorice":** I... don't know.
**"Castorice":** I did it out of sympathy, believing that I wouldn't be racked with agony, but... I am still in a state of turmoil.
**"Amunet":** Sympathy? Sympathy for that youth who triumphed in every battle?
**"Castorice":** That young man was favored by Talanton, who taught him a move that will defeat any mortal enemy with a single blow and emerge victorious every time.
**"Castorice":** He could rely on that move to always achieve victory during Era Chrysea. However, now that death has descended upon the mortal realm, that divine swordplay also means he can send others to death at will. In other words...
**"Amunet":** You feel that he's similar... to you?
**"Castorice":** Yes. Since he can never be inferior to others in martial combat, death would be the inevitable outcome every time he strikes.
**"Castorice":** That's why he approached me and pleaded... Pleaded for me to put an end to all of this.
**"Amunet":** You are perhaps feeling lost from making your peace with "Death" in so abrupt a manner.
**"Castorice":** I... have not made peace with death.
**"Castorice":** I've seen the people of Aidonia stand tall and proud in the face of death. Even before the fated finale arrived, they were already content with the lives that they'd lived...
**"Castorice":** They had such proud expressions... Expressions that made me believe "Death" would be an insult to these mortals if the concept is truly a power that a god had carelessly cast down upon the world.
**"Castorice":** Perhaps death's authority was never meant to exist in this world... just like how I shouldn't exist.
**"Amunet":** I won't refute your beliefs for now, but would you be interested in listening to a story of mine?
**"Amunet":** It's a fairy tale from foreign lands, told by the person who brought you to me in that ice field long ago...
**"Amunet":** ...A tale told by the Queen of Styxia.
**Mem:** What? W—W—W—Wait a minute...
**Mem:** That means... The girl born in the dragon's embrace... The personification of "Death" these souls talked about... That's you? Cas???
**Castorice:** I've already had an inkling for a while now... But I honestly have no memories of Styxia...
**Castorice:** My memories only begin... After Amunet brought me to Aidonia.

**"Queen of Styxia":** We had been inebriated in Phagousa's feast for too long before we began to seek divine-bestowed "Death," so that we could be freed from the whims of fate... However, that brought doom upon us.
**"Queen of Styxia":** Instead of summoning a supreme god, we encountered a monster that cut off the River of Souls. That detestable dragon Pollux denied all the dead from the gates of the nether realm.
**"Queen of Styxia":** Unable to rest in peace, these departed spirits engulfed Styxia like a roaring tide. As a result, my city and its people no longer exist...
**"Queen of Styxia":** That child is the only person whom I managed to save. Born within the evil dragon's abdomen, she has possessed the talent of "Death" since her birth. She must have been granted to us by the gods as a chance for us to atone for our sins...
**"Queen of Styxia":** Please, you must nurture her to adulthood... and prevent her from experiencing all of the suffering that we went through.
**"Amunet":** ...I promise that I will care for her as a mother, that I will protect her from the evils of "Death."
**"Queen of Styxia":** By Phagousa above, thank you for your kindness...
**"Queen of Styxia":** As her mother, please bless this child with a name... Before I am swept away by the River of Souls, please let me know how I should address her in my next life.
**"Amunet":** ...
**"Amunet":** Like how a transient raven butterfly, a fleeting papilio castor, perches on the twig of "Death"...
**"Amunet":** "Castorice"... That shall be her name.

> **Castorice:** The first time I wore a dress personally made by Lady Aglaea... the first time I went for a walk with Lady Tribbie, Lady Trianne, and Lady Trinnon... the first time I enjoyed the night breeze and looked up at the stars with Miss Hyacine... the first time I took care of dromases with (Trailblazer)...

### Part 107 — `databank/missions/chapter-03-through-petals.md`

**Context:** Complete Dialogue > Sub-Mission 8: Ferryman, Ferry Me Across the Stream of Souls > Continue Exploring Dragonbone City and Proceed Upward with the Chains of the Past


**Castorice:** According to that alchemist, the final piece is supposed to be me...
**Castorice:** However, he is not privy to the past and therefore cannot see the full picture.
**Mem:** ...The past?
**Castorice:** I saw the twins of "Death" in Kephale's memory, and now I am certain that I was one of them.
**Castorice:** The trial of the "Hand of Shadow" demanded "taking the life of your family." I died during that trial, falling before the gate to the new world... And my sister, whom I never met, became the Titan Thanatos of this cycle.
**Castorice:** She was consumed with regret over my death, so she used the Titan's authority to reforge my soul. Yet, it also means she has never revealed herself to anyone, hidden away from the entire world...
**Castorice:** That's because the Titan she became already fell at the start of everything... They died for me and now lie in slumber in the River of Souls.
**Mem:** You mean... Pollux?
**Castorice:** That's right. The River of Souls is no material river. It cannot be blocked by ordinary means. Only a divine corpse can prevent the dead from reaching the next world.
**Castorice:** And that's why we have to awaken Pollux from death before we can retrieve the Coreflame of "Death"...
**Castorice:** We have to reforge its body with the power of "alchemy."
**Mem:** But Cas, if that alchemist speaks the truth... does that mean you're also part of the dragon body?
**Castorice:** According to those departed spirits, I was indeed born as the dragon's twin...
**Castorice:** But before we talk about that... Have you heard the sighs?
> *(Trailblazer)* I did when I was collecting the souls...
> *(Trailblazer)* You sighed many times on the way here...
> **Castorice:** No, not from me. The sounds that come from the memories when we were collecting the souls.
> **Mem:** Ah, I heard them too! So, what are those?
> **Castorice:** I think... it's probably the sign of Pollux's wish being fulfilled.
> **Castorice:** They must have realized how heavy a price they'd need to pay when they used their divine authority to resurrect a dead woman. That's why they exerted every ounce of their strength to send me to Styxia, a city that called for "Death" — A land where none is hostile to "Death."
> **Castorice:** They never saw their wish fulfilled and fell so prematurely, all because they wanted to give me a new life... Pollux must really want to know how their sister has been after giving their all to save her.
> **Castorice:** That wish is the reason Pollux's soul still lingers in the mortal realm even after their fall, quietly watching and documenting every detail of their sister's life...
> **Mem:** Ah! So, instead of picking up objects on the way here...
> **Castorice:** ...We were instead showing things to a strand of yearning that lingers here. Now we must weave these yearnings together and restore them into Pollux's complete soul.
> **Mem:** But all of this is just a hypothesis, right? What if we go through with the ritual, and it turns out totally different?
> **Castorice:** That's fine. As Professor Anaxa often reminds us, such is the nature of alchemy. The true outcome can only be known once the process is complete. This is why alchemy is also known as the "Miracle of Creation."
> **Castorice:** Now, the puzzle of Pollux's yearnings is nearly complete, with only one final piece remaining —
> **Castorice:** I shall reveal what they have been yearning to know in the alchemical matrix — I will show them my whole life since I left Styxia...
> **Castorice:** ...Lady Trianne has already drawn a picture of my entire life, and it is now in my hands.
*(Obtain Trianne's Hand-Drawn Picture)*

### Part 120 — `databank/missions/chapter-04-fall-at-dawn.md`

**Context:** Complete Dialogue > Mission 2 — Scrolls, Turn the Blade's Gaze > What's Mem Looking At!?


**Mem:** Uhh... Month of Cultivation, Curtain-Fall Hour, a time to feast on grains, but a poor time for fishing... But then again, are there even any spots left to fish in Amphoreus these days?
> *(Trailblazer)* What are you looking at? / Are you into fishing? / Superstitions, superstitions, don't buy into them.
> **Mem:** Whoa, you scared me! ...(Trailblazer), bursting into someone's world like that can really catch them off guard! Well, no more jokes. There's... there's nothing here! I'll go look elsewhere.
**(Trailblazer):** (Something's odd about Mem. What are they flipping through?)
**Mem:** These cards are so detailed and fine... "Ruler"... "Weaver"... "Traveler"... "Healer"... Hmm, but the interpretation for each card is so long. Do I have to memorize them all?
> *(Trailblazer)* What are you looking at? / "Ruler" fits me pretty well. / Use them flexibly. Don't just memorize.
> **Mem:** Ah! (Trailblazer)? Hmm... What a coincidence! We meet again. Guess we're fated to be together, huh? But you must be busy, so I won't bother you for now. Hope your day — every day — is joyful!
**(Trailblazer):** (So suspicious, not like their usual self...)
**Mem:** ... You sure are persistent. Alright, alright — I'm totally helpless against your charm...
**Phainon:** What're you guys chatting about? Is this... the *Amphoreus Divination Compendium*?
**Mem:** Why are you here too?
**Phainon:** Haha, sorry, seeing you two chasing each other around, I suddenly found my book not that appealing anymore. Never thought Mem would be into divination... But it's not exactly a niche hobby, so why all the secrecy?
**Mem:** I wanted to set up a surprise — master it in secret and give you all a shock! But getting caught halfway through, it's only natural I'd feel shy. A girl's heart is more delicate than Aquila's ever-changing moods...
**Phainon:** Ha, a "surprise," huh? Someone else used to tell me the same. She was really good at stargazing and divination, especially with cards — yeah, just like the ones you're looking at.
**Mem:** Then you must be pretty familiar with all sorts of divination techniques, with a friend like that.
**Phainon:** No, I know nothing about it. I've never really believed in using cards or bones to predict the future. If the good omens foretold by divination were true... my hometown, Aedes Elysiae, wouldn't have disappeared. Don't forget, we've got Lady Trinnon. Every other form of divination is just smoke and mirrors or cleverly rehearsed tricks compared to her flawless prophecies.
> *(Trailblazer)* A little faith in these things isn't bad sometimes. / It should be approached with a critical eye...
> **Mem:** To me, divination could be a power that guides our spirits. Fate always casts a shadow in front of us, clouding our view of the future. That's why people turn to divination. It's just to find a small reason to give them courage to face the unknown...
> *(Trailblazer)* You've really thought about this, haven't you?
> **Mem:** After all, the first chapter of the "Divination Compendium" discusses "the significance of divination." I'm just expanding on that a bit!
> *(Trailblazer)* What if divination predicts something bad?
> **Mem:** Divination is only meaningful when there are both good and bad outcomes. Even bad omens serve as guidance. They can help keep us alert, right?
**Phainon:** ... Strange... That person I knew used to say similar things. Looking back, whenever she interpreted a card for me, I couldn't help but feel uneasy. Even though I never believed in the outcomes. Maybe it was her tone? Cyrene... she could paint a complete fantasy so vividly, it utterly captivated me as a child. Right. I still carry a card she once used. Do you want to see it?
**Mem:** Wow... it's so exquisite! I'd love to have a card like that.
> *(Trailblazer)* Those mitts up to the task? / Then, gotta craft a Mem-specific deck.
> **Mem:** Oh, now that you mention it, yes! It would be even better if it were made of, say... crystal!
**Phainon:** Speaking of which, (Trailblazer), there's something I haven't shared with you all. Remember our first trip to the Temple of the Three Fates? The riddle that Oronyx posed to us... ...! ...Who's there!?

*(The party continues through the Eye of Twilight, activating more celestial globes to unveil history. They witness: New Believers persecuting Old Disciples; the Skyfolk conspiring with the Cleaners against Seliose; and finally Seliose's judgment upon her people.)*
**Memory Solabis:** Seliose touched the Coreflame and gained the vision of the Titan.
**Memory Lunabis:** She saw what the god fears — rooted in the darkness, spawning from the edge of the world, the black tide that devours all.
**Memory Solabis:** **They flee from the clouds not out of favoritism toward the scorching sun, but merely out of fear of that boundless shadow.**
**Memory Lunabis:** Yet humanity misinterpreted the god's intentions, forging them into weapons for fratricide... This history, brewed from sorrow and tragedy, shall end here.
**Memory Solabis:** We are here to deliver the message Seliose left for mortals — "To my once beloved, yet now unsavable kin — With golden divine blood coursing through me, even the Tyrant of the Sky bends to my unyielding light. My light burns far brighter than before, and the fog clouding my mind has finally lifted —"
**Memory Lunabis:** "I gaze upon this fragile earth through the god's hundred eyes, and resolution fills me. A hero's instinct drives me forward, compelling me to deliver your final judgment —"
**Memory Solabis:** **"Your future flows between the boiling streams of the golden pool at your feet —"**
**Memory Lunabis:** **"Rejoice, for as everyone's flesh and blood merge with liquid gold —"**
**Solabis & Lunabis:** **"You will finally understand what true unity means."**

**Cyrene:** Wait a minute... we actually look quite alike, don't we?
**March 7th:** Yeah! Your pink hair is just as eye-catching as mine!
**Cyrene:** And we're both bright, pretty, and positive girls! Talk about coincidence!
**March 7th:** Wow, you're quite the smooth talker... but I can't disagree!

**March 7th:** You know what I'm going through?
**Cyrene:** I know because I've been exactly where you are.

**Cyrene:** That door leads to Amphoreus. Trust me, it's anything but welcoming.
**Cyrene:** For ages, I've watched people walk through that door. They couldn't see or hear my calls. The voices beyond always promised safety, but I've never seen anyone return safely...

**Phainon:** Yeah, all the kids would get so excited drawing cards like "Ruler" or "Warrior," but throw fits over "Devil" or "Drunkard" and demand a redo.
**Phainon:** If you just redo it when you don't like the result... what's the point of divination?
**Cyrene:** Ah, the blessing of childhood. Believing the gods might not notice our little corner of Aedes Elysiae, willing to let our small deceits slide...
**Cyrene:** Still... fate is absolute. After all, we never really know just how far into the future the prophecy extends.
**Phainon:** Anyway, let's go to our old "secret base" and look for those oracle cards—
**Phainon:** Um... now where did we hide those...
**Cyrene:** I can think of a few spots... Let's go check it out.
*(Unlock Achievement "Through the Petals in the Land of Repose")*

**Phainon:** Well... probably.

**Cyrene:** Let your thoughts flow like stars drifting on the river, carried by fireflies and gentle winds, your destinies flowing towards the same future...

> **Phainon:** Such elegance... The Gold Weaver must be of noble birth.

> *(Trailblazer)* Reveal the "Gatekeeper."
> **Cyrene:** This is the "Gatekeeper," who guides people to their true path among myriad passages.
> **"Gatekeeper":** We have surrendered our countless senses, dedicating ourselves to the sacred whispers within the temple!
> **Phainon:** Wait... It almost feels like I can hear the echoes within a temple! And... are those children singing?
> **Phainon:** It feels overwhelmingly solemn, yet strangely comforting...
> *(Trailblazer)* Reveal the "Ruler"

> **"Ruler":** Even should you pledge your loyalty to me as the last sovereign, the glory shall be mine and mine alone...
> **"Ruler":** He who has slain a king can no longer bear the title. Let the withering lion walk his last path alone.
> **Phainon:** Is this showing us... a "Ruler" who forsook their people?
> **Phainon:** Stories of such bloodshed like this feel so distant from Aedes Elysiae... but they sure can stir the imagination.
> *(Trailblazer)* Reveal the "Traveler."
> **Cyrene:** Ah! The Traveler, our little trickster! Always hiding behind other cards, disguising itself. What a rare catch!
> **"Traveler":** ...Over here?
> **"Traveler":** Or perhaps — here?
> **"Traveler":** Gotcha! You'll have to be quicker than that!
> **Phainon:** That teasing voice... just like a child playing hide and seek. Surely, only a master of mischief could pick up this card!
> *(Trailblazer)* Check the other cards.
> *(Trailblazer)* Turn to the second page of oracle cards.
> *(Trailblazer)* Reveal the "Servant"

> **Cyrene:** It symbolizes new life and hope, yet often brings to mind struggles, sadness, and loss.
> **"Servant":** These precious flowers I care for with my own hands... are they destined to bloom only in death's embrace?
> **Phainon:** It sounds so solemn, yet tinged with an inescapable sorrow...
> *(Trailblazer)* Reveal the "Healer."
> **Cyrene:** The humble "Healer" may seem gentle in hue, but its courage matches any other, strong enough to melt even the coldest ice.
> **"Healer":** I will do my best to help you mend all fractures and let sunlight shine on every corner of the world!
> **"Healer":** Make a pinky promise with me. No more going back on your word!
> **Phainon:** Hey, this card really speaks to me. It makes me want to protect as much as I possibly can...
> **Phainon:** Still, "protecting everything everywhere" seems like a dream — what amazing power would you need for that?
> *(Trailblazer)* Reveal the "Scholar."
> **Cyrene:** Behold the Scholar, bearer of many names: the Grand Magician, the Great Performer, the Eye of Wisdom, and the Defiler of Cerces.
> **Phainon:** Oh, there's just one card? With all those titles, I was expecting a whole deck...
> **"Scholar":** "A dromas draped in finery" will suffice — it's succinct enough to best summarize my true nature.

> *(Trailblazer)* Check the other cards.
> *(Trailblazer)* Reveal Phainon's oracle cards.

**Cyrene:** In fact, it's the only oracle card without a dark side.
**Phainon:** ...
**Phainon:** What? No divine whispers this time?
**Cyrene:** Oh silly... this is simply the card you drew for yourself.

**Context:** Complete Dialogue > Mission 3 — (Side) Hero, Return to That Peace of Home > Talk to Cyrene About the Future Revealed by Oracle Cards


**Cyrene:** "Deliverer"...

> **Hysilens:** "Dux Fragoris" Labienus and "Dux Brumalis" Seneca are both formidable warriors, clearing the way for the Flame-Chase with steel and storm.
> **Hysilens:** Dux Fatorum, the Holy Maiden who interprets the prophecy... Only the one named Tribbie remains in the city. The Imperator once took them as hostages in order to unify the prophecies of the world... But they are now Okhema's most vital prophets.

### Part 191 — `databank/missions/chapter-06-before-their-deaths.md`

**Context:** Complete Dialogue > Sub-Mission 2: Sun, Repel Stars and Pale Moon > Meet Up with Hysilens


**Epos Representative:** Good sir from Paphas, what do you think the Imperator is planning, calling this meeting so early? My homeland can't endure anymore...
**Paphas Representative:** Who knows? First she wants to hunt Titans, now she says there are even greater calamities... Her madness is the greatest calamity.
**Ladon Representative:** Exactly! Chrysos Heirs, "Deliverers"... What about us ordinary folk? How much are we worth in her eyes?
**Cyrene:** Things feel pretty tense here...
**Cyrene:** The history books said that the Imperator aimed three cannons at Dawncloud to make all the delegates obey her. Will history repeat itself in this cycle?
**Hysilens:** Yes, similar acts have occurred, and not just once. However, whether history will repeat itself... you'll soon see.
**Hysilens:** Those are the two spots reserved for you. Please take your seats.
**Epos Representative:** All rise! In the names of Talanton and Kephale, the Okhema Alliance swears upon the gods...
**Cerydra:** Cease the pageantry. I pray not to the vanquished.
**Epos Representative:** What...
**Cerydra:** Kephale, of course, needs no further explanation. As for the subjugation of the "Law," at the Entry Hour two days past, a scout should've brought the news to all of you...
**Cerydra:** Unfortunately, guards found his corpse among the rocks not far from the city.
**Cerydra:** Moreover, the poor soul was buried with a Paphas curved blade. It pains me to imagine what agonies this loyal warrior endured before his death...
**Cerydra:** Delegate from Paphas, do you have any insights?
**Paphas Representative:** I—I know nothing of this...
**Cerydra:** The evidence is irrefutable, so there is no need to waste your breath. However, let us set this trial aside for the time being...
**Cerydra:** Witness! Even within the most sacred and dignified Council, there were those among us who wished to draw their blades, brazenly challenging the alliance, seeking to shatter our hard-earned peace and unity...
**Cerydra:** Fortunately, I uncovered this plot. Now, to protect this beautiful city and her people, the Imperator's legions are ready...
**Cerydra:** They will escort each of you to the Temple of the Three Fates, where you will receive the highest level of protection the alliance can offer.
**Cerydra:** Then, the legions' warriors will join me in embarking on the campaign against Phagousa.
**Paphas Representative:** This... this is clearly a trap!
**Ladon Representative:** Imperator! Did you summon us here just to trample on the dignity of your allies!?
**Cyrene:** Things are starting to get pretty intense...
**Ladon Representative:** Comrades, you've all seen her true face now! According to the ancient laws of Okhema, only with the consent of two-thirds of the Elders can the legions carry out the commander's orders...
**Cerydra:** Heh, the ancient laws! Didn't you destroy them long ago? You defected during the First Siege of Okhema, voted unanimously in favor of the Council of Elders' restructuring, and just yesterday, you desecrated them with a blood-stained blade...
**Cerydra:** The decaying old laws fell with Talanton and are no longer deserving of respect. But, if you still insist on proclaiming yourselves defenders of the "Law"...
**Cerydra:** Then be silent, and offer up the highest respect to this Coreflame in my hand.
**Cyrene:** That really is the Coreflame of "Law" in her hand...
*(narrative)* The representatives fell silent
**Hysilens:** Since there are no objections... then the original agenda of this meeting — "the campaign against Phagousa" — is hereby passed.
**Hysilens:** The Flame-Chase Army's hunt for the Ocean Titan begins today. In order to prepare for war, all allied city-states must surrender command of their militaries and pay all war taxes by the end of the Month of Evernight.
**Cerydra:** At this moment, I would also like to introduce to everyone the newest honored guest to join our alliance...
**Cerydra:** The "Deliverer" revealed in the prophecy of "Time," (Trailblazer), has descended from beyond the sky. With his aid, we shall shatter the Chalice of Plenty...
**Cerydra:** And return Phagousa's Coreflame to the holy city!
**Ladon Representative:** Absurd... simply absurd!
**Ladon Representative:** A Deliverer from beyond the sky? This is just like that prophecy about slaying gods... It's a lie you've used to grow your power!
**Ladon Representative:** Ladon has already sacrificed countless soldiers... And now you want me to send even more of my brothers and sisters to be used and discarded by a tyrant?
**Cerydra:** Hah...
**Cerydra:** Are there any other objections?
**Cerydra:** Then, I bid you all farewell... Meeting adjourned.
**Cerydra:** Deliverer, please wait...
**Cerydra:** I still have important matters to discuss with the two of you.

> **Cerydra:** ...
> **Cerydra:** Your advice is acknowledged.

> **Cerydra:** So-called trust and loyalty are but empty words to me.
> **Cerydra:** From this moment on, prove yourself through your actions. As long as no boundaries are crossed, I fully respect the choices of my subjects.
> **Cyrene:** May your journey reach that sky full of stars, Great Imperator.

> **Crude Ladonian:** The... The delegate from Ladon has been assassinated! Quick, quick, someone come quick!
> **Hysilens:** Ah, the broken crab shell surfaces. I was worried it had been washed away by the current.
> *(Trailblazer)* It was you, wasn't it?
> **Hysilens:** Just a regular cleaning, no need to be so surprised.
> *(Trailblazer)* I'll inform the guards!
> **Hysilens:** Please, go ahead. But let me remind you: The Commander of the Imperator's Imperial Guard and the Okheman Civil Guard is right in front of you.
> *(Trailblazer)* You're a vicious fish.
> **Hysilens:** Viciousness is a form of praise to the blade...
> **Cyrene:** ...I see. No wonder there was a faint smell of rust in the air before I even entered.
> **Cyrene:** Why? Is it because the Ladonians refused to contribute troops to the Flame-Chase Journey, or... just because they offended the Imperator?
> **Hysilens:** Because the new era of the Flame-Chase doesn't tolerate stagnant blood. For the ignorant masses who cling to outdated laws, only bloodletting can wake them up.
> **Hysilens:** Right now, the rebel forces on the frontier are rising, and Ladon has been secretly supporting them. They loudly chant the slogan "Return to the Era Chrysea," unwilling to relinquish military power. They're just building momentum for the rebellion.
> *(Trailblazer)* Fair enough.
> *(Trailblazer)* I reserve judgment on this.
> *(Trailblazer)* It seems a bit too extreme for me to accept.
> **Hysilens:** Thinking is a right given to citizens by the Imperator. I won't interfere with your thoughts.
> **Hysilens:** As long as you don't try to reverse the current of the times... you and I will both be free.
> **Cyrene:** But, Miss Hysilens...
> **Cyrene:** I... can't hear even the slightest hint of joy in your words.
> **Hysilens:** ...Because I've already tasted ultimate freedom.
> **Hysilens:** Long, long ago, I lived in the sea. In that lightless abyss, far beyond civilization's reach, the great fish consumed the small, and when the great fish perished, their bodies became sustenance for the newborn, nourishing the whole ocean.
> **Hysilens:** This is the tide that carries civilization forward: Anyone may be devoured, or meet the fate of inevitable decay.
> **Cyrene:** Are you suggesting... that ultimate freedom is in fact the cruelest fate for life?
> **Hysilens:** I only mean that if life wishes to preserve its dignity, it must first be bound by the "Law."
> **Hysilens:** ...There's no need for me to say more, little sea hare. We are of one mind.
> **Hysilens:** Whether the Imperator's "Law" can guide the schools of fish and lead us to a truly free ocean... let us wait and see.
> **Hysilens:** But for now, let's pause this heart-to-heart. Another fish has come, following the scent of blood.

> *(Trailblazer)* You will weave the most romantic thing in the world...

> *(Trailblazer)* You will bathe in the golden light of hope...
> **Aglaea:** ...

> **Aglaea:** But destiny's fragments are like stitches. Before they're threaded into splendid garments, no one can see their true charm, can they?

> *(Trailblazer)* What... What did you do...?
> **Cyrene:** Ahh...!
> **Hysilens:** The Dew of other gods all intoxicate humans, but the God of Banquets' Dew...
> **Hysilens:** Is an "antidote" that can awaken humans from a sea siren's enchanting song.
> **Hysilens:** Since the start of this banquet, all the guests have been under the spell of my song. None of them can see the Imperator and that Theoros.
> **Hysilens:** But you two... I wish for you to see the truth.
> **Cyrene:** Is this... also what the Imperator wants?
> **Hysilens:** This is my personal request. I... can't bring myself to trust that Theoros.
> **Hysilens:** The Flame-Chase Army changed after he descended with Oronyx's divination and brought the prophecy of the stars to Okhema. His seductive whispers harbor something malicious within and will ensnare those of us blinded by ambition.
> **Hysilens:** You are the "Deliverers" of the prophecy and know about him far more than I do. Perhaps only you can find traces of the truth from his actions and words...
> **Hysilens:** Then, we can show the Imperator the right path forward.
> **Cyrene:** But... isn't this acting behind her back?
> **Hysilens:** Amphoreus has become far too weak. Even heroic actions protecting her from harm must be conducted under the water's surface. I am the Imperator's blade, and I must clear the way for her while remaining concealed in the torrents...
> **Hysilens:** Then, ensure I'm pointing my sword towards the true enemy.
> **Cyrene:** ...
> **Cyrene:** Can you promise me something, Miss Hysilens?
> **Hysilens:** Ask away.
> **Cyrene:** (Trailblazer) has in fact come here to stop that villain's evil scheme. Our goals are aligned.
> **Cyrene:** But as to the decision the Imperator must make... (Trailblazer) has no wish to transgress her authority. We just want to tell her everything we know.
> **Cyrene:** I believe the Imperator will be able to tell right from wrong.
> **Hysilens:** I never doubted your loyalty. I'll pass your words faithfully on to her.
> **Hysilens:** ...Provided we can gain the advantage in this tug-of-war, that is.
> **Hysilens:** Now, return to the feast. Bring this cup with you and keep your distance. Pretend you are still immersed in the sea siren's fantasia...
> **Hysilens:** And I'll control the moisture in the air. That way, you'll be able to listen to sounds from afar, as if you are a whale of the depths, and hear every word of their conversation.
> **Hysilens:** I wish you a victory in this first skirmish.
*(Obtain Hysilens's Ceramic Cup and Dew of Divine Blood)*

**Lygus:** That so-called "Deliverer" is part of that last group. Those vagabonds mistakenly believe they can connect the stars through alliances instead of conquest, but history has already given them a tough lesson.
**Lygus:** There are countless other organizations besides, but when the Lord Ravager sweeps across the cosmos, they will certainly all bow before your empire of Destruction.
**Cerydra:** ...

**Cerydra:** ...If those civilizations are not as feeble as you describe, then they may still have some value in testing the quality of my blade.
**Cerydra:** That being said, even the smallest and weakest city-states should build an obelisk to the Imperator, so that their people can constantly recall the glory of her conquests.
**Cerydra:** Walk a little longer with me, Theoros.
**Cyrene:** I don't like how this is going. Our little Imperator seems fascinated by the Destruction...

**Herta:** Once you've dealt with that troubling old Intellitron, don't hesitate and start the next cycle of extrapolation right away. Screwy said that "Era Nova" isn't a process that happens in an instant, and the protected nodes of the Scepter are probably at their most vulnerable only during the reset—

**Aglaea Etched in Memory:** Its name is "solidarity." You must escape with your life and carry word of my death to those still fighting, then keep going with them.
**Aglaea Etched in Memory:** Just as the Imperator lit the torch of resistance with her death, my passing will make this flame everlasting.

### Part 215 — `databank/missions/chapter-06-before-their-deaths.md`

**Context:** Complete Dialogue > Sub-Mission 4: Zephyr, Uplift Bygone Dust Cloudsward > Go Forth. Resume the Unfulfilled Cause


*(Approach the memory of Trianne)*
**Trianne:** Keep moving forward. Little Gray, see you tomorrow!
*(When Cyrene activates the Fragments of Recollection)*
**Cipher Etched in Memory:** That seamstress wasn't wrong when she said a lie needs more lies to keep it together...
*(Approach the Fragments of Recollection)*
**Anaxa Etched in Memory:** Reason and Trickery...
**Anaxa Etched in Memory:** Never thought we'd be the pair to finish this final setup.
**Cipher Etched in Memory:** Can't catch that Tin Man without your little sleights. Besides, this is our last shot. Not the time to be picky, right?
**Anaxa Etched in Memory:** Don't oversimplify it. Lygus just found out Okhema's been an empty shell for the past fifty years. He's bound to be furious. At this rate, he could've done anything.
**Cipher Etched in Memory:** Ha, that's exactly what we want! Rage blinds even the brightest minds. Doesn't matter how much of a genius he is, the principle still holds. Once he storms into the Council and confronts me head-on...
**Cipher Etched in Memory:** You trigger your alchemy with a BOOM and turn him into a caged beast.
**Anaxa Etched in Memory:** Easier said than done. You still have to pass yourself off as the Deliverer without anyone catching on...
**Anaxa Etched in Memory:** And you really think a human alchemical array can bind a godlike being? That's just nonsense.
**Cipher Etched in Memory:** What, getting cold feet? Or you just don't believe in the almighty worth of the demigod of Trickery?
**Cipher Etched in Memory:** Just tell me straight: can you do it or not?
**Anaxa Etched in Memory:** ...
**Anaxa Etched in Memory:** Hmph. Of course I can. And it'll be child's play.
**Anaxa Etched in Memory:** But I've got two conditions. Since this is the final act, if we're doing it, we go all in. No half-measures.
**Cipher Etched in Memory:** Oh? I'm listening.
**Anaxa Etched in Memory:** First, shift the battlefield to the Vortex of Genesis. It would be more convincing if (Trailblazer) were to appear there.
**Anaxa Etched in Memory:** Besides, the Vortex is cut off from the world. No place in Amphoreus makes a better prison.
**Cipher Etched in Memory:** If you pull that, wouldn't our Deliverer have to face tank Lygus as soon as they come back?
**Anaxa Etched in Memory:** That clash is inevitable. All we can do is create more chances for him. And that's the second condition...
**Anaxa Etched in Memory:** The Vortex belongs to Phagousa in the first place, remember? And there happens to be a Demigod of Ocean among the few survivors in the world.
**Anaxa Etched in Memory:** **Loop Hysilens in. Let her join the final act. I'll transmute myself into a Philosopher's Stone and embed myself into the formula...**
**Anaxa Etched in Memory:** **And when the time comes, she needs to shatter me and scatter that stone's ashes into the ocean within the Vortex.**
**Cipher Etched in Memory:** ...Heh. I guess people weren't wrong when they said you're insane, Grove boy.
**Anaxa Etched in Memory:** I've got no idea how to outwit a reclusive genius... But making an Antikytheran suffer? The Demigod of Reason has ten thousand ways to do just that.
**Anaxa Etched in Memory:** And what I just mentioned was one of them.
**Cipher Etched in Memory:** So what exactly does your formula do?
**Anaxa Etched in Memory:** Now that would spoil the climax, wouldn't it?
**Anaxa Etched in Memory:** By now, Hyacine must've completed the Sky people's ritual.
**Anaxa Etched in Memory:** Since that Theoros loves a good performance as much as I do, let's make sure he enjoys our show to the fullest.
**Anaxa Etched in Memory:** As for how the "Deliverer" will complete the "Era Nova"... well, that will no longer be our concern.
*(narrative)* ...
**Cyrene:** "It was the last battle known to the world, initiated by two demigods least suited for war."
**Cyrene:** "In the end, Reason and Trickery kept their word. At the cost of their lives, they sealed Lygus within the Vortex of Genesis, where he remains to this day."

**Context:** Complete Dialogue > Sub-Mission 4: Zephyr, Uplift Bygone Dust Cloudsward > Go Forth. Continue the Long Journey


**Cyrene:** I'll tell you the story of this thousand-year journey. That way, it won't just be my memories alone.
**Cyrene:** And I'll share the moments I spent with (Trailblazer) with everyone. That way, the path we walked becomes a memory shared by all.
**Cyrene:** People will know that there's nothing left to chase. Amphoreus has already welcomed the dawn foretold in prophecy.
**Cyrene:** Just like I said, this won't be harder than the last time, right?

> *(Trailblazer)* Cyrene... even you...
> *(Trailblazer)* Is this... all a memory?
> **Cyrene:** ... Someone as sharp as (Trailblazer) must've realized by now that this isn't reality, but a maze of Time.

> **Cyrene:** And then... may it set sail in peace... Drift down the river of time along with the Coreflame of Worldbearing, reach (Trailblazer)'s side, and lead you back to Amphoreus.
> **Cyrene:** Just like the first time we met, right?
> **Cyrene:** Even though this cycle has no demigod of Time, Time has always stood on humanity's side.
> **Cyrene:** This is the only way to make sure that the "Era Nova" won't happen before (Trailblazer) returns...
> **Cyrene:** And the only way I can safely entrust the completed final Flame-Chase Journey to the Deliverer's heart.

> *(Trailblazer)* Let's go. It's time to complete the "Era Nova."
> *(Trailblazer)* I will take all of you to the new world.
> **Cyrene:** I believe (Trailblazer) will never let us down... just like always.
> **Cyrene:** Do you remember when Phainon sent us back to the beginning? When (Trailblazer) and I got separated? But in the end, I returned to you, carrying all that we've been through.
> **Cyrene:** That's what "memory" is. It may wear down and fade in the river of time... But it never truly leaves. When that hidden corner of the heart is stirred, the past returns even more beautiful than before.
> **Cyrene:** So don't worry. Let the 33,550,337th Flame-Chase Journey become part of (Trailblazer), too. I've gotten a lot better at it with the experience from the last time.
> **Cyrene:** Next, (Trailblazer) will head to Styxia and find the Vortex's entrance guarded by the demigod of Ocean, before charging toward the final battlefield.
> **Cyrene:** Don't worry. I'll be with you. All of us will. Even Phainon. We'll stand with the "Deliverer."
> **Cyrene:** So say a short goodbye, then set off, just like we promised at the start...
> **Cyrene:** ...Toss away all unnecessary worries and troubles, and bring our smiles and determination into tomorrow ♪

**Context:** Complete Dialogue > Sub-Mission 6: Sea, Bury the Wine-Dark Dreams > Go Forth, to the Silent Graves


*(Approach the gravestones)*
**Lygus's Voice:** Look, a gravestone...
**Lygus's Voice:** But whose name does it bear?
*(Reach the marked spot)*
**Lygus's Voice:** It was here that the prisoner, who thinks herself the prison warden, made her choice...
**Lygus's Voice:** Witness, listen... and awaken.
**Lygus's Memory:** Dux Gladiorum... I've lost count of how many times you've stood here over the past centuries.
**Hysilens's Memory:** ...
**Hysilens's Memory:** You talk too much for a prisoner, Lycurgus.
**Lygus's Memory:** Two geniuses who defied "Law" and all the Chrysos Heirs who sacrificed themselves in this cycle worked together to chip away my strength, but they — you — can never affect my will.
**Lygus's Memory:** And as long as my will remains intact, I am free.
**Hysilens's Memory:** Heh... don't get ahead of yourself. I still have ways to chain your divine soul.
**Lygus's Memory:** Please. Do surprise me. But I'd say, your mission is nearly done here, isn't it?
**Lygus's Memory:** It was here you gorged out the Coreflame of Ocean from Phagousa's body. It was here you and that Imperator both completed apotheosis, becoming pillars of Amphoreus. And after that...
**Lygus's Memory:** **You drove your blade into her heart, a loyal subject turned regicide, a demigod committing deicide, or so the history books paint that tragedy.**
**Lygus's Memory:** But if that's the truth, then why, after a thousand years, are you still here, guarding the Imperator's grave? Still bound by loyalty?
**Hysilens's Memory:** ...
**Lygus's Memory:** Come to think of it... perhaps I'm not the only one imprisoned here.
**Hysilens's Memory:** Your calm does stir the rage in me... As if the one paying the price here isn't you, but us, the ones who lost everything.
**Lygus's Memory:** Hehe... That's only because I've waited thirty million lifetimes for my vision to be realized. Countless millennia.
**Lygus's Memory:** Loneliness never once showed me its fatal side. In fact, for a prisoner of the cave, it's more like an old friend. One that helps me think.
**Lygus's Memory:** But you're different, Dux Gladiorum. Loneliness has crept from the silent depths, sinking its claws into your mind. Bit by bit, it's peeling you apart... Will you regress into one of those lost, mad sirens? Oh, what a shame that would be...
**Hysilens's Memory:** You're right, Lycurgus. Loyalty, curse, the fall of my kin, and this endless waiting... these shackles are too heavy for a fish that cannot perceive any light.
**Hysilens's Memory:** I can't break free of the whirlpool that's pulling me under. Nor do I have the unwavering resolve to force myself to wait for that person's return, as everyone hoped for.
**Lygus's Memory:** Ah... Deliverer from beyond the sky. My executioner. How much longer until she arrives here?
**Lygus's Memory:** Or... will she ever return?
**Hysilens's Memory:** I don't know. And I doubt anyone does.
**Lygus's Memory:** Then, what will you do?
**Hysilens's Memory:** ...

### Part 222 — `databank/missions/chapter-06-before-their-deaths.md`

**Context:** Complete Dialogue > Sub-Mission 7: Captives, Behold the Expanse Beyond Light > Find a Way to Defeat Zandar


*(Light Calendar 3960, after the feast)*
**Herta:** Screwy. What do you think?
**Screwllum:** If your hypothesis of Lygus's identity is true, then even if we remove the restrictions of the Ultimate Protocol, the chances of us defeating him in a head-on battle will still remain lower than the acceptable threshold.
**Screwllum:** Therefore, victory will still hinge upon Mr. (Trailblazer)...
**Screwllum:** And Miss Cyrene.
**Herta:** Go on. Maybe we're thinking about the same thing.
**Screwllum:** Lygus's cognition resides in the Exomyth. He is both an instigator within the experiment and the observer outside of it.
**Screwllum:** Logic: His Inspiration Circuit can simultaneously process the different time flows in and out of that world.
**Herta:** That's where things get tricky. He simultaneously exists in two different timelines where the internal clocks are wildly different. That old Intellitron's neurons have completely gotten used to that over this prolonged experiment, and that's why he holds home-field advantage.
**Herta:** You and I should be able to manage that too, if given more time. Unfortunately, there's no "if," and we don't have more time.
**Herta:** That's why we need to change our way of thinking and turn his advantage into a weakness.
**Cyrene:** The key lies in "memories," right?
**Herta:** Oh? You caught on quick.
**Cyrene:** You've already mentioned (Trailblazer) and I, so it's an easy connection to make, no? My intuition has always been very on-point.
**Herta:** Good. Then I'll cut to the chase: Since his thinking speed far exceeds that of normal people within Amphoreus, we are going to externally plant a "memory" into his Inspiration Circuit...
**Herta:** Then command it to loop endlessly until he gives in.
**Screwllum:** Even the most flexible and sturdy Inspiration Circuit cannot withstand the traffic of a full-on information flood. When the amount of information exceeds his cognition filter's capacities, he will have no choice but to focus on processing this memory.
**Herta:** That's when the parallel processing advantage he gained via Amphoreus will completely destroy him. An image from a person's memory played in real-time might only be a second for us, but for him in Amphorean time? It could be millions or even billions of calculations...
**Herta:** He won't be able to do anything but watch as he's locked into this prison forged by "memories."
**Cyrene:** **Then we can entrap him in the same kind of cycle as the heroes were in, and get him to experience loneliness and pain in that endless recurrence...**
**Cyrene:** It's my greatest honor to be on the same page as the geniuses.
**Herta:** (Trailblazer) is still in that old Intellitron's hands and won't be able to respond to us anytime soon, but I don't think she'd refuse. So, it's up to you to figure out how to execute this plan.
**Herta:** I'm not sure how this happened, but I knew it as soon as I saw you. You are currently a "memetic entity" of the Remembrance.
**Cyrene:** Yes. I seem to be something you folks beyond the sky call a **"memosprite,"** but that's fine by me. It suits me perfectly. I can serve as the vessel of memories and do my part for the final battle.
**Herta:** But before that, we have to tell you...
**Herta:** The Path characteristic you exhibit is very special. There's no way you're just a simple "memosprite." However, I'm sure about one thing...
**Herta:** You are a memetic entity born in Amphoreus, yet you only awakened with (Trailblazer)'s arrival, and only matured as she embarked on the Path of Remembrance.
**Herta:** That means your existence is completely attached to (Trailblazer)'s memories. If there ever comes a day when she forgets about you for even a few seconds or loses her last connection with Amphoreus...
**Herta:** Then you must be prepared for the worst. You will disappear in the Memory Zone, utterly gone, without even a single line of data to remember you by.
**Cyrene:** ...
**Cyrene:** But that's already happened, hasn't it?
**Cyrene:** No wonder I've always had a weird hunch. But that also means I was right in thinking...
**Cyrene:** As long as (Trailblazer) and I are together, we can do anything.
