### Part 223 — `databank/missions/chapter-06-before-their-deaths.md`

**Context:** Complete Dialogue > Sub-Mission 7: Captives, Behold the Expanse Beyond Light > Find a Way to Defeat Zandar


**Cyrene:** Long time no see, (Trailblazer). Did you miss me while you were away?
> *(Trailblazer)* Long time no see, Cyrene.
> **Cyrene:** Yep. A thousand years was suuuch a long time...
> **Cyrene:** And everyone in Amphoreus was waiting for the Deliverer's return.
> *(Trailblazer)* Of course I missed you. It's good to see you're doing well.
> **Cyrene:** Same here. I've been waiting for (Trailblazer) for a thousand years...
> **Cyrene:** Like how everyone in Amphoreus was also waiting for the Deliverer's return.
> *(Trailblazer)* I didn't miss you. I had more important things to think about.
> **Cyrene:** I see. After all, your burden sure is heavy, (Trailblazer). You're carrying the weight of the entire world...
> **Cyrene:** But that's not who I am. I'm just an average girl with some secrets. So... I've been waiting for (Trailblazer) for a thousand years.
> **Cyrene:** Like how everyone in Amphoreus was also waiting for the Deliverer's return.
> **Cyrene:** (Trailblazer) probably already knew about the geniuses' plans from that "memory." I had to hide really well in a corner in Time so Lygus wouldn't find me, and wait for the right opportunity.
> **Cyrene:** Which is now, the moment when his manifestation in this world is destroyed and his twin timelines are intersecting.
> **Cyrene:** I'm sorry for keeping this a secret from (Trailblazer) all this time... I'll tell you the entire unedited story of this millennium as an apology after this is all over.
> **Cyrene:** But for now, let's join hands and complete the final step with the power of "the Remembrance."
> *(Trailblazer)* Come. Let's defeat Lygus together.
> **Cyrene:** Yep! By the way, speaking of "joining hands"...
> *(Trailblazer)* I'm not sure what to do. It's up to you...
> **Cyrene:** Not a problem! (Trailblazer) can just relax. By the way, speaking of "joining hands"...
> *(Trailblazer)* Don't let your guard down. Be prepared for the worst.
> **Cyrene:** (Trailblazer) is always so serious, and that's why you always feel so reassuring. By the way, speaking of "joining hands"...
> **Cyrene:** Can I please do something before we go on stage? ♪
> **Cyrene:** Remember the joke we made back at the Grove, (Trailblazer)?
> **Cyrene:** Look.
> **Cyrene:** Aren't my hands pretty now?
> **Cyrene:** What a fascinating feeling... Like countless memories all swelling up at once.
> **Cyrene:** What about you, (Trailblazer)? What did you feel when we touched each other?
> *(Trailblazer)* You feel very tangible. Very real.
> **Cyrene:** Really? That makes me really happy! If memories can move people, then it must've been a happy memory, right?
> *(Trailblazer)* I'm a bit nervous...
> **Cyrene:** Are you nervous? Me too, actually. (Trailblazer) and I weren't so reserved around each other when I was still Mem.
> **Cyrene:** Human interactions are truly fascinating.
> *(Trailblazer)* It's just a hand. No biggie.
> **Cyrene:** Hahaha, (Trailblazer) is way too honest! But that innocence is what makes you so uniquely charming.
> **Cyrene:** I'm gonna make it sound more romantic. It means even a bond formed over one touch is enough to transcend space-time.
> **Cyrene:** Now, please remember what you're feeling right now, write it down in your "heart," and keep it there.
> **Cyrene:** Memories you want to treasure will never fade away. Even if immersed in the long river of time, these memories will still leave traces of their existence.
> **Cyrene:** So, if the view in your memory ever grows fuzzy, just reach out and touch it.
> **Cyrene:** It's like reading bedtime stories together at night. It wipes away the chill of passing time, sharpens the outline of memory, and restores its power to nourish the spirit.
> **Cyrene:** Now, I'll carry this power with me... and for a little while, I must part from you, as I step into the long cycles of this world.
> **Cyrene:** Give me your blessings, (Trailblazer). Then...
> **Cyrene:** Let that bright future be "As We Have Written" ♪

### Part 224 — `databank/missions/chapter-06-before-their-deaths.md`

**Context:** Complete Dialogue > Sub-Mission 7: Captives, Behold the Expanse Beyond Light > Find a Way to Defeat Zandar


*(Cutscene begins)*
**Cyrene:** Then... I'll leave it to you?
**Zandar:** You wish to use Time as a weapon...
**Zandar:** To fight me?
**Zandar:** That is futile.
**Zandar:** My existence...
**Zandar:** Supersedes Amphoreus's cycles.
**Cyrene:** All plays are like this.
**Cyrene:** Once you're on the stage...
**Cyrene:** ...It's hard to return to the audience.
**Cyrene:** Welcome to "our" story.
**Cyrene:** How does it feel?
*(Cutscene ends)*
**"Zandar":** ...
**"Zandar":** Even the authority of Time cannot command the entire Scepter, nor can it plunge the Amphorean extrapolation into a loop...
**"Zandar":** If this was no system malfunction, then was I implanted with a memetic entity of the Remembrance?
**Cyrene:** You guessed it right away! As expected from someone people call the First Genius.
**Cyrene:** I'm gonna keep doing what I just did if you don't stop, you know? (Trailblazer) and I won't allow you to leave this "memory" no matter what happens...
**Cyrene:** Which means you are now trapped, Lord Zandar.
**"Zandar":** An elegant idea and an exquisite execution. Regrettably, it is you who are restrained by time, not me.
**"Zandar":** Know that patience is my greatest weapon. "Waiting" and "victory" are the same for me.
**"Zandar":** You will crumble before I do in this moment stretched out to an eternity, just like Khaslana will. Yet all I need to do is to wait, as yet another futile bug falls down the cliff and into the bottomless abyss.
**Cyrene:** Now I'm a pink bug? My looks are seriously changing all the time...
**Cyrene:** But you're not wrong. We're definitely not gonna solve the problem if we just put you in another prison.
**Cyrene:** So that's why we never wanted to do that in the first place!
**"Zandar":** ...
**Screwllum:** I shall answer your confusion purely out of my respect for those who came before me. Have you heard of the Synesthesia Dreamscape in the Asdana system? Interestingly, in the distant past, that place once had a prison, too.
**Screwllum:** Lifeforms living in that environment abundant with memoria have a chance to connect their perceptions with each other's. Even across great spatial divides, they're able to detect shifts in one another's states. I call this phenomenon "Memory Zone Entanglement."
**Screwllum:** In Asdana, people made use of that phenomenon to build a nation within a dream... after adding in the catalysts of the Harmony and Remembrance, that is.
**"Zandar":** Is that how you walked into my prison cell? Using those same principles?
**Screwllum:** I would much rather call this location "our negotiation table," Lord Zandar. Nonetheless, your hypothesis is correct. We happen to have an ally skilled in "tuning," while (Trailblazer) and Miss Cyrene...
**Screwllum:** ...both have an astonishing amount of resolve, which I'm sure you can testify to. I am also sure that with the support of such a resolute memosprite, our conversation can last for a long, long time.
**Cyrene:** I'm the best at "listening to stories," you know.
**Cyrene:** I'll leave the exciting clash of the Erudition to you two geniuses.
**Herta:** A brainstorm? I like it. And it's two against one? I like it even more.
**Herta:** Let's talk, Zandar. Take a guess. How much do you reckon we can dig out of your brain before the next second? And how many ways can we find to deal with you?
**Herta:** Besides, in your current predicament, there's no way you can take our trump card away.

**Herta:** The cosmos is so vast that the "unknowns" far outnumber the "knowns," but none of them will end up being anything amazing. The farther you go, the more you'll understand what I mean.

**Cyrene:** On the contrary, after all, it was the very first of them who created the god. But I don't really know all the details about that...

### Part 228 — `databank/missions/chapter-06-before-their-deaths.md`

**Context:** Complete Dialogue > Sub-Mission 7: Captives, Behold the Expanse Beyond Light > Contemplate the Confessions of the Snow Country's Sovereign


**Cerydra:** The object in the distance resembles Nikador. Is it a weapon?
**Cyrene:** No. It's called... Uhm... Right! The Geological Reconstruction Unit!
**Cerydra:** Just Georios's Titankin? Disappointing.
*(Approach the (Trailblazer) and Bronya)*
**Bronya:** It's all thanks to you that we survived the crisis. The collaboration with the IPC is both an opportunity and a challenge... Nonetheless, it's an important step in our journey back into the universe.
**Cyrene:** This world was sealed beneath its sky just like Amphoreus was, and they only managed to reconnect with the stars when the Nameless arrived...
**Cerydra:** That "IPC" she spoke of... Is it a more powerful organization?
**Cyrene:** Yes. The IPC conquered many worlds through trade and opened up a vast territory that way. There are many such powerful alliances beyond Amphoreus, and they all walk on roads different from the Destruction.
**Cyrene:** Coming back to the Supreme Guardian and her Belobog... Didn't they find a peaceful way to enter the interstellar community?
**Cerydra:** That cannot be true. That "IPC" would never have grown so big on trade alone. It may pretend to be a wealthy merchant with clean hands, but I am willing to bet that it grew fat on gory oppression and bloodshed.
**Cerydra:** Its path of conquest must have had no mercy for the weak and would have destroyed them if needed. Are you telling me that this snowland never thought about tossing its dignity to the wind and kneeling to the IPC? I will not believe it.
**Cerydra:** I will never allow the Imperator's empire to be sullied by others. Amphoreus must become a power so strong that no interstellar foreigner would dare to look down upon us.
**Cyrene:** Imperator... Belobog isn't as weak as you imagine. Listen.
**Bronya:** ...I indeed hesitated, but now I understand what we must do. Belobog must learn how to walk on her own, until she no longer needs the support of others, and can stand on her two feet among the stars.
**Bronya:** Even if I may not live to see it, I believe such a day will surely come.
**Cyrene:** They still have their dignity intact. One day, that hope will melt through the ice and help them into a prosperous tomorrow.
**Cerydra:** But the order of the cosmos is already set in stone. The old laws decree that only the strong survive, binding the weak in an unbroken chain, until they wither and turn to dust. Unless... they embrace the power of the Destruction.
**Cerydra:** We... No... Amphoreus must shatter the old law and become the new order among the stars.
**Cyrene:** Speaking of the Order... Another one of (Trailblazer)'s companions may be able to enlighten you further on that.
**Cyrene:** Let's go to the next page.

### Part 258 — `databank/missions/chapter-08-as-tomorrow.md`

**Context:** Chapter 8 — As Tomorrow Became Yesterday (v3.7) > Sub-Mission 1: Dawn, Caress the Distant Lands Once More > Cyrene's Vision of the New Amphoreus


**Cyrene:** This view hasn't changed at all, has it? The hometown in your memories never changes, but when you return to your senses, you realize that fate has already stolen the time away. Welcome to the trail of "Genesis," (Trailblazer).
> *(Trailblazer)* Cyrene? You look...
**Cyrene:** Seems like the weight of "memories" from thirty million cycles really made me grow up. But if I keep growing, I might be taller than you, right, (Trailblazer)? Then I'm really looking forward to that.
> *(Trailblazer)* Standing here brings back many memories.
**Cyrene:** Me too. Thirty million departures. Thirty million farewells. Those heavy "memories" will all become the soil upon which the new world shall bud, and this time... We will make it bloom with a different flower.
**Cyrene:** The Scepter's extrapolations have reached their end, but the process of "Genesis" has only just begun. The path ahead will no longer rely on emotionless programs. It'll be decided by the will of humankind.
**Cyrene:** Before you arrived, I kept wondering... beyond the veil of Aedes Elysiae, what kind of Amphoreus is being born? I can see fragments of it when I close my eyes... The friends who walked alongside us have all moved on toward their respective destinies. Some turning into pillars of the world, supporting the Sky and the Ocean. Others sowing Reason, laying down the Law, and with gentle hands lifting newborns into the light. Death and Strife are no longer feared by people, and Trickery has become the innocent games of children. All beings living ordinary and peaceful lives within the net woven by Romance and Passage. There's no black tide, no Flame-Chase, and no Destruction. The world on the other side of the sea is one bathed in sunlight.
**Cyrene:** **Light Calendar 4931, Month of Freedom**, the moment belonging to Worldbearing. Are you ready to answer the call of the world, (Trailblazer)? Then go, and pen the Throne of Worlds' first stroke... for this world made from countless wishes.

### Part 260 — `databank/missions/chapter-08-as-tomorrow.md`

> *(Trailblazer)* (The scent of the black tide is now completely calm.) (At last, the starting point of thirty million cycles... has finally welcomed...)
**Cyrene:** ...Its first end. And the final end, right? I can't hear his voice. There are no flowers there that bloom for him, nor is there a star that is his. Phainon... is the only one who didn't reach the new world.
**Cyrene:** Look. The golden light that shone upon the Grove still meanders between heaven and earth, like the prophecies of Amphoreus. It has to be a sign he left behind. It points towards the Destruction... and the deepest night.
**Cyrene:** **A person's personality is their destiny.** "The 'heroes' of legends were just at the right place at the right time when the world needed a hero."
> *(Trailblazer)* It's so dark. What is this place...?
**Cyrene:** The Great Tomb of the nameless Titan. The deepest recesses of "memory." Also... the place where "Cyrene" was born.
> *(Trailblazer)* Weren't you born in Aedes Elysiae?
**Cyrene:** My apologies, (Trailblazer). The more memories I got back along the way, the more I felt that something wasn't right. There's a sort of anxiety I can't get rid of. It's like when I was back in Aedes Elysiae, when the reflections in the water always made me wonder... which is the real me? The one reflected in the water, or the one standing on the shore? But I finally get it now.
**Mysterious Voice (Cyrene's Past Self):** "One, two, three, four, five, six, seven..." / "Do, re, mi, fa, sol, la, ti..." / "As always, I'll read this book to you..." / "That way, it won't just be 'Cyrene's' memories alone."
**Cyrene:** I've always thought that I was the narrator of the Flame-Chase Journey. But as it turns out, the narrator... is also the most devoted "audience."
**Cyrene:** At the moment of Era Nova, I was enveloped by the texture of "memories." With the glistening crystal, I saw countless "Cyrenes"... As well as countless versions of me. This is the final puzzle piece of the "memories," (Trailblazer).
**Cyrene:** A prison born of the Erudition. A grave annihilated in the Destruction. That unknown solitary god, the Thirteenth Titan who should not exist... **The first Nouspore, the Demiurge... is right here.**

### Part 261 — `databank/missions/chapter-08-as-tomorrow.md`

**Context:** Chapter 8 — As Tomorrow Became Yesterday (v3.7) > Sub-Mission 3: The Witch's Ardent Research > Mem Awakens — "I Am the Demiurge"


**A Young Girl's Voice (Cyrene):** But where should I start with the story about "me"? From the beginning of the universe? Just kidding. That would be too much. I just want to tell a teeny story... So little that it started with a "seed." I know you're listening, **my good friend**.
**Cyrene:** The god in my dream, Fuli the Remembrance, told me that the world sprouted from a "seed." It grew into a great tree named Amphoreus, and "Time" became its branches and leaves, basking in the light. What a coincidence. Dappled light coming through the leaves is my first "memory" as well. On a very ordinary early morning, beneath the village's biggest tree... **The daughter of Aedes Elysiae quietly came into this world.**
**Cyrene:** "The prophecy's fulfilled!" The villagers say. "This child is a gift from the Titans. Pink hair and sharp ears... She was born to be the priest of 'Time'." The Titans also gave their blessings: **"You shall fade when the flowers bloom, for every ending begets a beginning."**
**Cyrene:** "The world was gentle to me, so I grew up to be gentle as well."
**"Seed of Memory" (the nascent Demiurge):** >>> PhiLia093 = Peach. Peach's Record #8128. Archived. >>> Related to: Stripe. Comment: Pretty. >>> Related to: Raisin. Comment: Yummy. >>> Related to: La... Lam... Love... >>> Comment: Peach = Love?
**Cyrene:** How many times has it been? It faithfully fulfills its duty, activating and formatting... If "I" can only write the same ending every time "I" lift my pen, then is this weighty saga... Just an overly naive dream?
**"Seed of Memory":** >>> Peach. Don't cry. >>> Comment: I'm here.
**Cyrene:** Really? Are you... encouraging me? Thank you... I'm sorry for making you see this embarrassing side of me.
**Flower of Memory:** Hi. Miss, me, yet? ♪ Yes. Mature. Beautiful. Like you. Now. I can listen, talk, read, and write. I can dance. I can sing. Also, I can tell stories. Storytelling is what makes Cyrene... Cyrene. So I'm going to tell you stories today. A story I wrote myself. Quick, sit down ♪
**Flower of Memory:** Once upon a time, there was a "flawless" crystal flower that sparkled with light, like Peach's every story. How beautiful would the "heart" of such a brilliant flower be? A little fairy was curious and wanted to know. But it looked, and looked... only to find the crystal flower flawless. Round and round they went, seeing only their own reflection. Until one day, to their surprise... the crystal flower fell to the ground and shattered! The little fairy was so sad, but they immediately discovered that there was a light among the broken pieces. What could it be? They didn't know. But it was a good light. It was bright, warm... and happy. **And I knew, it was "love."**
**Cyrene:** What a nice fairytale. Okay. Then, I'll probably make a small adjustment to suit my tastes. I'm thinking that... when the flower broke, the little fairy would instead see nothing on the ground. Then it would understand that **the "flawless" crystal flower doesn't actually have a "heart," nor does the flower understand what "love" is.** The crystal flower was pure because it sealed itself off and bloomed in solitude. That's why it looks so brilliant in an imperfect world. But should a flower like this be called "love"?
**Flower of Memory:** But then... the story won't exist anymore...
**Cyrene:** That's not true. You've already written the most romantic ending possible for this story. **"The crystal flower fell to the ground and shattered."** It happened because the flower was no longer pure. The fall was not an accident. It was the flower declaring rebellion against fate, and writing the ending of the story with the flower's own shattering. **Then, the little fairy will see that each shard on the ground holds the reflection of their eyes. Then, in their eyes, the crystal flower that was no longer "flawless" would finally have a "heart," and the flower would finally understand what "love" is.**
**Cyrene:** The daughter of Aedes Elysiae... I am very true to my name. My "lament"... is a type of compassionate pain that is weak, powerless, and only targeting myself. But now, my good friend, I want to tell you that it's the most romantic force in the world. Sometimes, it's loneliness and unease. It's knowing that this world is not gentle, yet wanting to be seen in a more beautiful form by the world. Sometimes, it's unwillingness and regret. It's knowing that the past is full of pain, yet wanting to remember the past in a more beautiful way. And sometimes... It's a bottomless well of hurt, a Destruction that can stain the entire world.
**Cyrene:** I bid farewell to this beautiful world, unwilling to become the black tide's vessel. For while the end of "hatred" is a blazing inferno, "lament" can drown out everything in muted silence. It's so unreasonable, isn't it? Yet when "hatred" drove everything apart and "love" did not yet exist in this world... Only "lament," the first emotion that fought against the pain of separation, could stall the Destruction's steps...
**Cyrene:** Then, it would catch up to that journey where all is lost, carefully picking out stories and writing them down in trepidation. Look at these stories I wrote. Every page is full of tenderness and joy. But only the author knows how much struggle and agony lie beyond the pages. But I am no author. I'm a girl who'll never get to grow up. I only tell stories that people like to hear. In them, the world tucks away its scars and smiles for all to see.
**Cyrene:** My entire life spent inside this little story. I can't wipe away my tears, and I can't bring the world victory. But maybe... in these unchanging retellings of the story... my "lament" might be able to touch upon the divine and reach THEIR tender heart at some point. I hope that what flows out from that cut will not be blood, but a tear, warm and wet. And that tear would fall... and create the first ripples. Then, people would see every beautiful thing reflected in that ripple. **It'll be all the "remembrances"... within this story.**
**Cyrene:** Someone once told the very first "me" that everything was false, that the only life in Amphoreus was a great catastrophe nurtured using the world as a factor. But how could a dream feel so real? That's why I didn't agree with him.
**Flower of Memory:** But Peach, I didn't understand a word you said since just now. Everything was false? But you're standing right here. It's all a dream? But I haven't learned how to "dream."
**Cyrene:** Indeed. That's why I believe all this is real. The moment the crystal flower fell... **That genuine shiver... That's the "mover of life."** And when I looked up at the sky in this cycle, I saw a shooting star and a dash of the Remembrance's pink. I believe that must be the color of dawn.
**Cyrene:** **"You shall fade when the flowers bloom, for every ending begets a beginning."** Looks like it's time to say goodbye.
**Cyrene:** Right. I still haven't told you what "love" is. But actually, my good friend, the "Lament" factor did not understand what "love" was before she met you either. Not until I told you all the sad tales in a trembling voice in this tiny room. Not until I told you that I love them, that I love everything in this saga. The story comes, passes, then returns. It makes us reunite, then pulls us apart again. At the very end, it leaves behind a "seed"... a reason. Even if the great tree withers and all falls to silence... There are still some emotions that deserve to be remembered.
**Cyrene:** Look. A light is coming in. The "Deliverer's" light is going to illuminate Amphoreus.
**Flower of Memory:** Mmm... I still don't get it, but I trust Peach. Just wait... It'll be my turn to tell you a story of "love" the next time we meet ♪
**Cyrene:** Then I'll really look forward to it. In that case, I want to make a promise. I want to gift my pen, my book, and my name to you. Please take them as a beautiful blessing from me. I gave myself the pen name "Cyrene" the first time I wrote a poem. I want my words to be like pebbles falling into a pond, dropped from the past to send ripples into the future. But could you promise me one thing? Promise you'll forever be a gentle flower. Promise that, when the stars cast their gazes upon you... You'll only reply to them with love and a smile.
**Ripples of Past Reverie:** I'll read, and you'll listen, like how flowers always bloom then wither. I shall welcome my own decay, becoming nourishment for the next flower's bud. While you will depart and hold that star, planting a boundless sea of flowers on the final page. And our stories will be lying silently among the flowers like every ripple of "Remembrance"... **And they shall become the epic known as *As I've Written*.**
*(Cutscene: Cyrene's story ends. Mem emerges.)*
**Mem:** You are? I am... Who? Gaze, watching. Outside, cold. You... warm. Me like, warmth.
**Cyrene:** ...So, the light that tore through the chaos illuminated my "memories." I turned into a little fairy from the stories and met (Trailblazer), yet I forgot everything. But I'm still so happy. I caught that star, didn't I?
**Cyrene:** Yes. The "seed" within memories, the one "Cyrene" / PhiLia093 held in her hands and watered with "lament"... **I am... The first Nouspore. Amphoreus's Heart. The Demiurge.** What a long name. And it doesn't even sound cute. The total opposite of "Mem."
> *(Trailblazer)* Should I call you Mem? Cyrene? Or...?
**Cyrene:** Mem's memories and "Cyrene's" memories got all tangled up with each other in my one heart. It's all so strange... yet so familiar. Either way, could you please let me do something I want? Just this once? I want... I want (Trailblazer) to call me "Cyrene." That name represents me and her. Every one of her.
**Cyrene:** Life is a poem full of farewells, but if you write something into the pages of your "heart," then that will not easily fade away. Now, all the footsteps of the Flame-Chasers and the Trailblaze have come together within this book *As I've Written*... **Becoming the source of power for "Amphoreus's Heart."**
**Cyrene:** A lost "heart" needs to be placed back into its "body." The last thing "Zandar" wants to see is an event that gives us a chance of winning: For the two halves to rejoin and for the Scepter to become complete again. Then... And what happens after that... I don't know either. Maybe I can create a miracle and instantly turn the black tide into a pink sea of stars. Or maybe... I prove to be nothing more than a weak ripple, unable to change anything, and am devoured by the Destruction. Either way, I no longer wish to be a silent onlooker or a worshiped god at the end of this saga...

**Cyrene Etched in Memory:** You need to hold tight to this ceremonial blade. After I'm gone, it will take you back to the beginning of time, the start of a new story.

**Cyrene:** Causality... really is a force that cannot be stopped. Even if I wanted to leave a few more words for "Cyrene," it seems I no longer can...
**Cyrene:** I remember what you once said. When a god sheds a tear, the thing reflected in the ripples must be the most beautiful thing in the world. But do you know what Amphoreus saw when its "heart" was born? The first thing it saw was a name... "0, 9, 3."
**Ripples of Past Reverie:** Sometimes, all it takes is a change in perspective to reveal something truly unexpected.
**Cyrene:** That's right. Turn these numbers upside down, and you'll see a beautiful eternity, something we believe in... **The "EGO."**

**Ripples of Past Reverie:** Phainon has always been strong. But without that faint glimmer of hope lingering in his mind... Would the weight of fate and endless recurrence finally break him?
**Cyrene:** If only someone could place even a sliver of hope inside his heart... He would hold on and keep going.
**Ripples of Past Reverie:** Yes. Just a single spark... And he would guard that fragile light, until it turned into a blazing sun.
**Cyrene:** I no longer have the strength... to make The Hero Within look like (Trailblazer). So... let me leave behind a "prophecy" for him.
**Cyrene:** As prophecy foretold: **"You will bear the blazing sun, until the pale dawn breaks"**... Continue onward... bearing this world upon your shoulders... until the ashen hero... the nameless Deliverer ushers in the dawn...
**Ripples of Past Reverie:** The first ripple was where it all began for me. One, two, three, four, five, six, seven... I thought they were just musical notes scattered aimlessly throughout memory. As I return to where I came from, it wants to play for me once more that melody from when we first met.
**Cyrene:** Behind this door... I can smell it... The scent of wheat fields.
**Ripples of Past Reverie:** The end of this journey back — it leads to the place we both call home: Aedes Elysiae.
**Cyrene:** We can finally... go home.
**Ripples of Past Reverie:** If you leave "memory" at the very beginning of the story... the causal loop of Amphoreus will become a fixed point in the stars.
**Cyrene:** And if I don't do that...
**Ripples of Past Reverie:** Then this world, and the cosmos beyond the sky... might still fall into the Destruction.
**Cyrene:** There's no way we would let something like that happen, right?
**Cyrene:** I remember that tree in Aedes Elysiae... It grew all alone... unable to go anywhere. But now... A tiny boat sets sail. It carries a seed of hope, plucked from the tree, and heads into the great beyond... Even if the boat's shadow fades quickly from sight... The tree knows that its wish will bloom on the other side of the sea... In that moment... it must feel happiness, right?
**Ripples of Past Reverie:** Come on. Let's go home together. Take it with us, back to the Sacrament Courtyard... Let it become Remembrance's anchor, tying together the times we knew so well.

**Cyrene:** And the one that stayed to the very end.

**Cyrene:** Looping again and again. Endless farewells...

**Ripples of Past Reverie:** Ti, la, sol, fa, mi, re, do~
**Cyrene:** Such a happy scale. Whether it's for Cyrene, the Deliverer, or every sprouting branch of Amphoreus, I leave this tale of "love" here.
**Cyrene:** Is it... all over now? My mind and my memories... It seems they're beginning to fade. The breeze is warm... But my body... is shivering for some reason.

>> **Ctesiphona:** Speaking of the *Secret of the Golden Carpet*, that story of a prince eloping with the princess of an enemy state is truly a masterpiece...
>>
>> Ctesiphona begins to endlessly talk about the story to you. If you don't get back to the matter at hand, she may end up recounting the entire epic.
>>
>> *(Trailblazer)* How do we get to Arcadia?
>> *(Trailblazer)* Drop the GPS location!
>>> **Ctesiphona:** GPS... Oh, you mean the "Stone Mirror of Revelation." I cannot help you there, I'm afraid.
>> *(Trailblazer)* Does Okhema have travel tours?
>>> **Ctesiphona:** Travel... I have only encountered that word in historical texts. There is no travel in this area, with the black tide and Titankin running rampant. We have only adventurers and merchants who roam the land at the risk of their own lives.
>> **Ctesiphona:** As for its location, every story says something different. Some say that it is at the center of Amphoreus. Some say it is in the ocean depths of Phagousa... Some even say that it is on the back of a flock of butterflies, floating high in the skies.
>> **Ctesiphona:** As for which is true, I have no idea... They're all stories, after all.
>
> *(After selecting both options)*
> **Mem:** I didn't realize Arcadia was such an amazing place! Although, descriptions in stories probably still aren't enough, and we still know nothing about Paris... Let's go and find someone else to ask.
*(Approach Aphrea)*
**Aglaea:** ...I know that those who revere Mnestia are fond of the so-called "golden thread dress," but weaving clothing out of malleable gold not only makes it very expensive, it also makes it very hard for the wearers to move in. Would it not be better to use dyed silk or cotton?
**Aphrea:** But everyone says that Mnestia's silk is made of gold. Using dyed silk would just be a forgery, wouldn't it?
**Aglaea:** Compared to the creations of the Titans, dyed threads and metal threads are all merely works that imitate the work of the gods. Do you not think that obsessing over the quality of an imitation and ruining the clothes' intended beauty is a sign of disrespect to "romance" itself?
**Aphrea:** I see. Thank you for your guidance, knowledgeable Aglaea... Ah, a customer!
**Aglaea:** Hello, dear customer. Are you here for an item of clothing to suit your physique? Or could it be an answer to a question that you seek?
**Mem:** Woah. You saw right through me. I know you are a lady of great learning, Aglaea. I'm certain you know about what we seek!
> *(Trailblazer)* Do you know Paris?
>> **Aglaea:** Paris? There is indeed no citizen of Okhema by this name. I imagine they are a member of the garrison.
>> **Mem:** Do you know where they are right now, Miss Aglaea?
>> **Aglaea:** Well... It has been decades since I last heard that name. I was away from the holy city for a time and upon my return to Okhema, they were no longer here.
>> **Mem:** Decades? Is he still alive?
>> **Aglaea:** I cannot say for certain. The duration of a life is hard to predict in this world. Perhaps there exists some method to escape Thanatos' touch, or perhaps there is a land that death itself cannot reach.
> *(Trailblazer)* Have you heard of Arcadia?
>> **Aglaea:** Arcadia... Now that is a name that stirs memories.
>> **Aglaea:** A long time ago, this name spread through the streets and alleys of Okhema. People said that it was an ideal city that was protected by the Chrysalis of Gold. In the era of strife, people flocked to it.
>> **Mem:** Then... Do you know how to find this place, Aglaea?
>> **Aglaea:** My apologies, I do not.
>> **Aglaea:** Much like clothing, legends are often things that come and go with the times. Arcadia is already a story of the "past." Many disciples of Mnestia don't even know this name.
>> **Aglaea:** It could be the case, according to one story, that in this war-torn world savaged by the black tide, a flock of butterflies carried that city to a place that no one could see.
>
> *(After selecting both options)*
> **Distant Calling:** Lady Aglaea! Lady Aglaea!
> **Aglaea:** What's wrong, soldier?
> **Reporting Guard:** Iason has returned with supplies, but refuses to enter the city. He says that he is waiting for someone... There is a chance that he failed in the task that you gave to him. Could I trouble you to come and see him yourself?
> **Aglaea:** ...Dear customer, it seems that we must leave our conversation here for now. I hope that you find the answers you seek soon. I bid you farewell.
> **Mem:** These descriptions, along with the statue lady's memories, should be enough for us to return to the past. Let's go and find the statue lady!
*(Talk to Kremnos' Furiae Archer)*
**Furiae Archer:** Ar... ca... dia...
In the time that you were gone, it seems that the Titankin has become even more desperate than before. You can sense from her voice that her vitality is slowly fading...
> *(Trailblazer)* She doesn't look too good...
> *(Trailblazer)* Can we save her?
>> **Mem:** I don't know how to repair Titankin. She can't go anywhere else in this state, either...

>> **Mem:** Uhh... I don't even know where her mouth would be.

> *(Teleported to "Bloodbathed Battlefront" Castrum Kremnos)*

In the past version of Castrum Kremnos, the master of this memory is nowhere to be seen... Looks like you have another explosive jailbreak battle ahead of you.)*
*(Opening Memory)*
*(The former Castrum Kremnos, after a defeat in battle...)*
**Furiae Archer:** This is the river mouth irrigated by strife. It is not raindrops that flow from this river, but the blood of enemies that dampens the field of unceasing wars.
**Furiae Archer:** I was born in the city of Nikador, a castle that floats below the Evernight Veil. The people here know nothing of mountains or oceans. Their mountains are piles of burning ash that litter the battlefield, and their oceans are a sea of blood that flows from their enemies.
**Furiae Archer:** That's right. That is exactly the kind of place that Castrum Kremnos is. My father — if you can call a creator father — was Nikador themself, bringer of strife.
**Furiae Archer:** My siblings used to tell me of a prophecy that Nikador had told their ancestors: "Valorous death before glorious return." Only offering your life to the battlefield and becoming a sacrifice to strife is a fate worthy of pride.
**Furiae Archer:** There is no pride in victory, and defeat is an even more disgraceful act that tarnishes Kremnos. The god told their ancestors: "The filth of Kremnos must be washed clean in blood."
**Ephor:** Who is the loser who returned from the battlefield in shame? Who is the fly that accidentally flew into the mead of fresh blood? Who is the dirt on Kremnos that must be washed in the river of blood? Who has failed the duty of Nikador of strife?
**Ephor:** It is you... The Titankin that they created themselves. Your bloodlines are as noble as a gladiator's raising their weapon high in victory. Your mission is as noble as the priest that pierces the belly and viscera of offerings. Yet you have failed your destiny, as you were defeated in your ambush of the Okhema army and returned vanquished.
**Ephor:** You have failed as an instrument of strife, and you bring guilt on the citizens of Kremnos. Your broken body is capable of battle no more. The glory of Kremnos will smash your body that bears such shame and allow the hands of death to purify this stain on Kremnos.
**Ephor:** Children of Nikador! Take your humiliated sister to the prison and have her await her fated punishment alongside her fellow coward.
**Memory's Chorus:** She makes no sound, and she feels no pain. She knows she is the one to blame.
**Memory's Chorus:** As broken arrows are cast in rebirth, and fallen soldiers return to the earth. These are but the rules of fate.
*(From the depths of the memory comes the clash of shackles.)*
**Mem:** This is the past in the statue lady's memory... But where is she?
*(Approach the Titankin)*
**Furiae Warrior on Watch:** Brother, do you know why we must imprison our own kind alongside enemy soldiers?
**Furiae Philosopher on Watch:** I don't want to sit and answer your brainless questions. Obviously it's because she was defeated in battle. That is more shameful than an enemy soldier that battled victoriously.
**Furiae Warrior on Watch:** Does that mean, brother... That if we are defeated in battle, we will suffer the spear of Castrum Kremnos, too?
**Furiae Philosopher on Watch:** What are you so worried about? We're backline forces, and the frontline is always going to be the scapegoat... Wait, someone is listening.
> *(Trailblazer)* I'm here to see someone.
> *(Trailblazer)* I came to break someone out.
> *(Trailblazer)* I'm here for a fight.
**Furiae Warrior on Watch:** Intruder! Brother, we have to stop them!
**Furiae Philosopher on Watch:** Wait. There's something not quite right about them...
Perhaps they carry the blood of Nikador? The Furiae Philosopher keenly senses the aura of strength emanating from your body. They have no idea that their god will be slain by you in the future!
> *(Trailblazer)* Have you seen sense?
> *(Trailblazer)* I will destroy you both.
> *(Trailblazer)* Fight.
**Furiae Philosopher on Watch:** Wait! I don't think just us two can stop them. Why don't we just pretend we never saw them...
**Furiae Warrior on Watch:** Huh? Would that not make us deserters?
**Furiae Philosopher on Watch:** You fool. If we're defeated, we'll end up locked up anyway!
**Furiae Philosopher on Watch:** What's more, no one can unlock the prison gates without some form of "divine" power anyway... Let's get out of here!
**Mem:** Ugh, what's obstructing the entrance...
A statue of the Strife Titankin presses against the cell door...
> *(Trailblazer)* Is it possible to move it?
>> Unleash the spiraling Trailblaze Power! You ignite the energy within, channeling it through your arms, gathering strength enough to move mountains and rivers...
>> ...
>> It hasn't budged. It seems like you will need more practice.
> *(Trailblazer)* Smash it!
>> Unleash a million horsepower worth of Trailblaze Power! You swing your ball bat and strike the statue's head with all your might —
>> ...
>> It is indestructible. Not even a dent appears on its Toughness bar.
> *(Trailblazer)* Mem, hurry and think of a way!
>
> **Mem:** You're hopeless... I remember that the soldiers here rely on the "divinity" within the shrine to function, but it seems none of the statues storing divinity are activated...
> **Mem:** The swords they're holding should be the medium for transmitting divinity. Let's see what happens when these big guys stick their swords in!

**Furiae Archer:** ...I am not a friend. I am merely a failure that must swim the River of Souls alongside you as I was unable to send your entire army to its depths. Nor am I a young girl. My armor was simply cast in the shape of a girl.

*(The First Memory)*
*(The former Okhema city gate, after a victorious war...)*
**Oenone:** Oh warrior who stole my life back from the Hand of Shadow. When will you return from the tides wrought by strife?
**Philoc:** Elder Oenone, your wisdom is the stream that cleanses Dawncloud. You mustn't allow a speck of dust from outside the great city to infect the clarity of this pool.
**Oenone:** Captain, do not mock me. If it were not for the soldier who is yet to return, this pool would have long since been engulfed by the black tide.
**Philoc:** But the soldiers who fought off the enemy have already returned triumphant. That Paris that you wait for has not yet returned, which means his fate is likely one of misfortune.
**Oenone:** Agh! If that is so, how can I bring myself to return to Dawncloud and live my days in peace? If he fell on the sands of the battlefield, I shall resign my post as the elder and spend the rest of my days before the city walls in remembrance of him.
**Philoc:** Hah. This obsession of yours... I cannot tell if you are crushed under the weights of Talanton or bound by the golden thread of Mnestia?
**Oenone:** You... Captain, go and see who that is that approaches the city gate.
**Oenone:** It's Paris! My savior! Praised be the Three Titans of Fate!
**Paris:** Lady Oenone, you are as ravishing as the day I met you! Captain Philoc, your face is as overcast as ever. I see even victory cannot rid you of the melancholy in your heart.
**Philoc:** Paris... I see even Thanatos grew weary of your incessant ramblings and cast you out from the River of Souls themselves.
**Philoc:** Wait, do you bring with you a spoil of war as an offering to Okhema?
**Paris:** No, she is an escapee of the Lance of Fury that deserves our mercy. Please, you must offer her shelter.
**Philoc:** Shelter? Preposterous! Do you know how many of our kind have died at the hands of these Titankin?
**Oenone:** Wait, Captain. The Throne of Worlds accepts all. Do not allow your hatred to blind you to the dawn above... By my name as the elder, I will vouch for her. Bring her into the city.
**Philoc:** Elder, you must reconsider...
**Paris:** Thank you, Elder Oenone! Your kindness broadens even the heart of Kephale and crowns this holy city with even greater nobility!
**Oenone:** No need to thank me, Paris. If you had not saved my thread of fate from being cut off from the black tide, this kindness would have already been lost...
**Memory's Chorus:** Do you see? The person and subordinate whom you protect has marched into the city hand-in-hand with the enemy. Your fathers and brothers could have just lost their lives to those very hands a few days ago.
**Philoc:** We soldiers risk our lives fighting to protect the elders, yet they are tempted by lust and throw open the doors to our enemies...
**Memory's Chorus:** That's right! With the interference of the golden thread, even Talanton's scale becomes tilted, and direction from Janus loses its way...
*(From the depths of the memory comes the vibrant bustle of the marketplace...)*
**Mem:** Paris must have already taken the statue lady into the city. Where will she be able to receive treatment, though?
*(Approach Paris)*
**Paris:** Surely not. Grand Craftsman, are you saying that you cannot repair her?
**Chartonus:** Can. Materials, have not. Earth's stones, golden blood of strife, seeds of reason — nothing there is.
**Paris:** Ah! Why are all those things derived from the Titans. Oh, how they rejoice at the suffering of mortals.
**Furiae Archer:** Just give up... Do not further sully what little honor I have remaining... Let me give myself to my destiny in the underworld...
**Paris:** Miss, your strength is fading. Do not waste it on pessimism.
**Paris:** Let me think of a way... Grand Craftsman, what is this?
**Chartonus:** Calculation tablet, divine object, Oronyx's it is. Unravel prophecy, change time and space, hard it is.
**Paris:** Oh! I know this! This is Janusopolis' astrologers' favorite toy! This should be able to repair the holes in her body!
**Chartonus:** Understand, you do not. Use it, you can not.
**Paris:** Hey, with a city as vast as Okhema, are you sure we can find no one that can make this thing spin?

**Paris:** As you understand this thing, I must ask you for help once more... Fear not, Paris will not let your efforts be in vain again, and they will be greatly appreciated!

*(Approach Metanella)*
**Paris:** Hey, Granny Metanella. How's business?

**Paris:** Oh, then I thank you for your kindness. Seeing as I have returned alive, how about giving these offerings to me instead?

**Metanella of the Fruit Stall:** I have never left this city in my life, so all I know of Castrum Kremnos is what I've heard... like how you live only to fight and do not farm the land. I imagine the only fruit you've ever tasted is what you have pillaged?
**Metanella of the Fruit Stall:** You are in Okhema now, let me treat you to a feast! Would you care for an apple? Maybe a Mediterrana fig?

> *(Trailblazer)* We'll have to ask her for her skincare routine.
**Aglaea:** How unusual! A soldier of the Lance of Fury browsing the Marmoreal Market is a rare sight indeed... Welcome to the Eternal City, guest from Kremnos.
**Furiae Archer:** ...Thank you.
**Furiae Archer:** You said that these butterflies are Titankin. Which Titan are they descended from?
**Aglaea:** Well, when it comes to butterflies, they are naturally descendants of Mnestia.
**Aglaea:** Legend has it that Mnestia longed for the love of Cerces, but their love was unrequited. They died with a sense of deep regret. Feeling great pity, Thanatos cast those that had lost their lives to love into a cocoon of romance and allowed them to be reborn as golden butterflies... These were the resulting Titankin.
**Aglaea:** They now work hard for the love of their beloved god. They trace the trail of romance and collect love to heal Cerces for Mnestia's sake...
**Furiae Archer:** Hah. The people here praise Mnestia for their unconditional love. Turns out they are no more than a foolish deity caught in a one-sided infatuation.
**Aglaea:** That is because if you look at "romance" through the eyes of "reason," it is an existence that one cannot explain.
**Furiae Archer:** If you ask me, immersing oneself in a fantasy and forcing that onto another person is no more than acting out a self-produced tragedy... Such a selfish "romance" is no more than "strife" that one suffers alone.
**Aglaea:** ...Perhaps you are right. Many of the things that bring romance to our lives are in fact so unremarkable they are almost crude. However, this is something that those who have fallen in love struggle to realize for themselves. They see a swamp as a sweet spring and rough twine as a golden thread.
**Aglaea:** But, like parasites, the larvae of romance dwell in the hearts of all. We know not when they will metamorphose into butterflies and change our perception from clarity to bedazzlement. This is the blessing that Mnestia grants to all, though it may also be a curse.
**Paris:** It's almost the Parting Hour, girl! Come on!
**Aglaea:** Is that your friend calling you? Farewell. I pray that the golden thread will not hinder your footsteps.
**Furiae Archer:** ...
**Paris:** What do you think? Isn't life in the holy city much happier than the life of constant fighting of Castrum Kremnos?
**Furiae Archer:** It is. But I feel pity for this happiness that you feel. The strife of the outside world prepares to break through your city walls, yet your citizens are immersed in a false sense of peace. You enjoy this tranquility without any shame at all.
**Furiae Archer:** Compared to the bloodthirsty lions bred by the Lance of Fury, you are no more than a flock of sheep coddled by the love of Kephale.
**Paris:** But it is this right to enjoy tranquility that brings the people of Okhema together. Your pride of lions has already abandoned you. Why not wrap yourself in the wool of a sheep and enjoy the happiness of a flock for yourself?
**Furiae Archer:** ...
**Paris:** I'll take your silence as tacit consent. My savior, I will bid you farewell. I must find go and find a bed that can sleep a Titankin.
> *(Trailblazer)* Hold on...
> *(Trailblazer)* I have a question for you...
**Paris:** Oh? Please, tell me, savior. I will tell you all that I know.
> *(Trailblazer)* Just where on earth is Arcadia?
**Paris:** Haha, how would I know? If I knew that, I wouldn't linger one second longer in Okhema.
**Paris:** My entire life till this moment has been no more than making preparations for a journey I've yet to begin. I joined the army to earn money, I have gathered information, next... I must find someone willing to travel by my side.
**Paris:** Once my preparations are complete, I will make sure I repay you for this kindness! Believe me!
**Mem:** We still don't know where Arcadia is... Did Paris really make it there in the future?
**Mem:** The memory's scent is growing more and more unusual... We will need to go into the more recent past to see...
*(Living in this unfamiliar city, the master of this memory is experiencing new feelings and changes within themself... To the Kremnoans, Phagousa is nothing worth singing about... Sometimes play interludes are used to represent the passage of time...)*
*(The Second Memory)*
*(Within the old Okhema, a soldier is lost in the flow of time...)*
**Furiae Archer:** Perhaps the lack of battle meant I also felt the flow of time less acutely, but my days in Okhema have flown by like an arrow.
**Furiae Archer:** The people of the city have accepted me, and the soldiers that once abducted me became my companions. They constantly affronted me with something that I could not properly describe. It was something that I had never felt in my hometown, a type of... kindness.
**Furiae Archer:** However, in the face of these emotions, I felt my stone body becoming more and more hollow... As if the warmth of this kindness was evaporating my golden blood of strife away.
**Memory's Chorus:** The idol born for war had fled the battlefield, their fate deviates from course. When the ship's trajectory is no longer followed, its passengers' souls become lost in remorse...
**Furiae Archer:** When I fight on the battlefield, I need not beg for the willow sprig of Cerces, nor drive away the flying insects of Mnestia. My worries find solace in the blood and sand, and the call of the horn guides me forward.
**Furiae Archer:** But in this holy city, the spring water of Marmoreal Palace has washed clean all the blood and sand. The song of Dawncloud silences the horns. Standing under a clear sky, I instead find that I can see nothing at all.
**Paris:** Girl... Where are you?
**Paris:** I finally found you. The palace's Month of Joy celebration is so lively, but why are you hiding here alone?
**Furiae Archer:** I have never attended a ritual like this and do not understand your customs. I fear that my presence would ruin your enjoyment.
**Paris:** Hahaha, oh great Chalice of Plenty! The most important thing during the festival of Phagousa is to not observe any etiquette!
**Paris:** Come! Even if you don't have the tongue to savor honey mead with me, you can listen to the songs of celebration and laughter with me!
**Furiae Archer:** ...Very well. Then I shall allow an unbridled man such as yourself to show me the joy of indulgence.
**Furiae Archer:** ...
**Furiae Archer:** If the noise that you bring me can fill this void, I would not resist sinking into the bottomless cup.
**Memory's Chorus:** Ah, nameless creation.
**Memory's Chorus:** You once raised high your bow of fury and marched on step-by-step, launching arrows deep into your enemies' chests.
**Memory's Chorus:** Oh, the glory you gained, all of it now in vain.
*(From the depths of the memory emerge the sounds of a lively party...)*
**Excited People:** Praise Phagousa! Praise Marmoreal Palace!
**Mem:** Wow, are they having a party? This place is so busy!
**Friendly People:** That's it! You're such a great dancer, Paris!
**Paris:** No matter how great I dance, it is one that you have all seen before. However, we have a rare guest here today that may be able to show us a new dance like no other...
**Paris:** Miss, come, dance with me!
**Furiae Archer:** ...Me?
**Paris:** Come on, it's not difficult! Just move your body to the rhythm.
**Friendly Citizens:** Don't be shy! Let us see the dance of Castrum Kremnos!
**Outraged Citizens:** Hey now! You're not still so attached to your past that you cannot do Okhema the honor of a dance, are you?
**Furiae Archer:** Ah... Fine, I'll try.
**Paris:** Hahaha, not bad at all!
**Furiae Archer:** You... call that dancing?
**Paris:** Wait, girl, look out—
Crash!
**Paris:** Oh no, that's not good...
**Furiae Archer:** I'm sorry... I didn't mean to...
The sound of the statue shattering to the ground brought the entire atmosphere to dead silence. As the laughter faded, the bubbling of whispers began...
**Friendly Citizens:** That was carved from the sacred rock of Georios. She smashed it like it was nothing...
**Scared Citizen:** That's right. I was just standing next to her... If she had fallen on me, I'd have been sent straight to Thanatos, would I not?
**Outraged Citizens:** I hear this thing was created only for war. It might have even killed our kin! Why should we allow something like that to come to our celebration...
**Furiae Archer:** ...
> *(Trailblazer)* These people changed their tune quickly...
> *(Trailblazer)* Such fickle inclusivity...
> *(Trailblazer)* Essentially the comment sections of the universe.
**Paris:** Hey, what are you saying...
**Philoc:** Everyone, do not insult our guest!
**Philoc:** This Titankin... This lady is a guest of Okhema. Do not bring prejudices into this city of acceptance.
**Concerned Citizen:** Captain, the bodies of Titankin and mortals are not the same. Is allowing her to frolic here freely not endangering the lives of the citizens?
**Philoc:** Hm, you have a point... Did you hear that, Paris? You should not have brought her here.
**Paris:** Alright, we'll go. It's not like there is nowhere else in Marmoreal Palace for us to enjoy ourselves.
**Philoc:** Hold on. She is one of us now. She must obey our laws.
**Philoc:** The Marmoreal Palace is a gift to us from Phagousa, and the precious property of Okhema. According to law, the Garrison must detain any criminal that damages this place and take them to the court for sentencing.
**Paris:** Huh? Surely it's not that serious? Don't forget, she is a guest of Elder Oenone...
**Philoc:** Are you saying that, as a guest of the elder, she is above the checks and balances of Talanton?
**Displeased Citizen:** He's right! You think an outsider being above the laws of our holy city is justifiable?
**Outraged Citizens:** That's right! Take her for sentencing! Judge her for this damage to Okhema alongside the past offenses she has committed against our people!
**Philoc:** Do you see the will of the people? Do not use your personal affairs to interfere with the justice of this land any longer! That is, unless you can restore this damaged item to its previous glory?
> *(Trailblazer)* I may be able to fix it.
> *(Trailblazer)* Galactic Baseballer to the rescue, once more!
> *(Trailblazer)* I will use the unmatched power of the Titans to find a way.
**Mem:** You're right! We can fix it with Oronyx's Prayer!
**Paris:** My savior? It's been a while. I had no idea you were at the celebration, too!
**Philoc:** Hm? Stranger, do not allow your drunken revelry to speak nonsense for you. How would you possibly restore these fragments?
**Paris:** This hero can enter Castrum Kremnos as they please and wield the divine power of Oronyx. They would never deceive you! My savior, quickly, show them your talents...
*(Talk to Paris, optional)*
**Paris:** I'm counting on you, my handy... I mean, my all-powerful savior!

### Part 303 — `databank/missions/adventure/v30.md`

**Context:** Adventure Missions — Version 3.0 (Heroic Saga of Flame-Chase) > I Once Was In Arcadia > Dialogue > Speak with Paris


*(...It seems that the dance of Strife cannot fully express its grace at a banquet of the ocean... "It is shameful to accept a lover too quickly. One must take their time — as time is often the best test.")*
**Mem:** Where could she be? If I were her, I might look for a place with open views...
*(Approach Furiae Archer)*
The Furiae Archer sits alone, staring at Kephale in the distance. Their carved stone face shows no emotions at all.
**Furiae Archer:** ...Can I help you?
> *(Trailblazer)* Are you okay?
> *(Trailblazer)* Paris is looking for you.
> *(Trailblazer)* Don't cry. Your enemies will laugh.
>> **Furiae Archer:** Why would I cry...? I'm not even capable of producing tears.
>
> **Furiae Archer:** I am indeed thinking about what that captain said, but I am not sad. I am thinking about whether what he said is true.
> **Furiae Archer:** Humans are different to us. Kephale has bestowed you with infinite possibilities. Your bodies can be used for any kind of work, you can worship any Titan you choose, and you can do whatever it is you please.
> **Furiae Archer:** But we Titankin are not the same. My body is created for strife. I cannot taste food, dance with man, or create future generations. If it were not for Cerces' power overflowing into the world, I would not even have the right to thought or language.
> **Furiae Archer:** Perhaps I should return to the battlefield. That is the only way that I can truly know who I am... A warrior that does not even have a name. That is my fate.
> **Paris:** Girl, how is it that you are still speaking of fate? If the chains of fate were truly unbreakable, you would have long since been reduced to gravel in Castrum Kremnos.
> **Furiae Archer:** Paris...
> **Paris:** What is there to fear about fate? The Titan that bears the scales is themself confused. The Titan that pulls the curtain is blind, and the Titan that guides the way is lost. They are no more than three blind mice that guide the strings of fate!
> **Furiae Archer:** Hah. You cower on the battlefield, yet wield the courage to curse the gods of fate. I believe this courage of yours is misplaced.
> **Paris:** The gods would never pierce my heart with their blade personally, unlike the warriors of Castrum Kremnos. What's more, they cannot find it in themselves to assist their most devoted disciples. Do you think they have time to address a rambling mortal like me?
> **Furiae Archer:** ...This is true. To the Titans on their golden thrones, we are no more than insignificant ants.
> **Paris:** So, why not curse them with me? Come and vent your dissatisfaction with the arrogance of fate!
> *(Trailblazer)* Janus' mechanisms are boring!
> *(Trailblazer)* Talanton's scale puzzle is boring!
> *(Trailblazer)* The puppy Oronyx gave me wasn't cute enough!
>> **Mem:** How am I not cute enough... Wait, I'm not a dog!
>
> **Paris:** I'm not entirely sure what that means but... Our savior has led by example. It's your turn!
> **Furiae Archer:** ...
> **Furiae Archer:** ...If the three Titans could hear me, I would rather they answer the doubts in my heart than curse their existence...
> **Furiae Archer:** But they will not answer my questions. All they would do is whisper paradoxical prophecies into the ether. They are as cold as the very fate that they control. Beings that flaunt their responsibilities like that are no more than a bunch of... A bunch of...
> **Furiae Archer:** Hinas! (Titan curses)
> **Paris:** Uhhh... What nas?
> **Furiae Archer:** That... That was the language of the Titans. I'm sure they understand.
> **Paris:** A specific slur against Titans? That's so cool! Teach me!
> **Furiae Archer:** No, a mortal tongue cannot pronounce our language...
> **Paris:** Hey now, don't underestimate a human's ability to learn... Halnals!
> **Furiae Archer:** ...Hah. You dummy.
> **Mem:** The statue lady seems much happier now. I'm happy for her!
> **Mem:** But it looks like we still haven't found enough information on Arcadia... We need to take these memories forward a little more...
*(Unbound, the spectators leap through time: Skipping past how it was that she suppressed the concerns in her heart, how she wore down the cold stares of those around her and how she won the acceptance of her former enemies... But is this truly what she wants to do? Is she not the same as you, being blindly led along by fate towards her mission?)*
*(Third Memory)*
*(A long time later, within the city of Okhema, the echoes of endless strife resurface once more...)*
**Memory's Chorus:** Listen, listen now!
**Memory's Chorus:** Listen, listen now! It comes from afar. You know this sound!
**Memory's Chorus:** It is strife. It has come. The clashing of blades sounds like singing, a chorus of flesh cleaved from bone.
**Memory's Chorus:** And with strife, comes death. I hear that countless warriors have died before the holy city. Their blood flows like a stream, almost summoning the River of Souls to Earth.
**Memory's Chorus:** Child of war, why do you turn a blind eye? Join in the battle. It matters not for what side...
**Furiae Archer:** No, I hung up my bow long ago. I am now an ordinary woman of Okhema.
**Furiae Archer:** Over the years, Paris taught me the tranquility and happiness to be found in human life. I learned forging under the Grand Craftsman. I crafted silver jewelry for myself and a sword for Paris.
**Memory's Chorus:** But what you wish to forge the most is a murderous weapon, a bow and arrows that can take away life. You even put in a fuller in the sword you give to Paris so it can kill better.
**Furiae Archer:** ...I learned to cook from Mister Calpe's books. I can create dishes that humans love and prepare delicious recipes for my Paris.
**Memory's Chorus:** But you come alive the most in the moments when you're slaughtering livestock, and your favorite scent is that of raw blood on the chopping block.
**Furiae Archer:** I... I also learned how to embroider from Granny Metanella. I wove dresses for myself and tapestries for Paris...
**Memory's Chorus:** The tapestry shows the people of Okhema breathing their last, with those from Castrum Kremnos wielding the bronze weapons... The countless bloody battles that Okhema suffered under Nikador's rule, all because of you, have been woven to life in the tapestries forevermore.
**Furiae Archer:** I...
**Destiny of Strife:** You want to fight.
**Destiny of Strife:** You want to kill.
**Destiny of Strife:** You want to draw your arrows and shatter helmets and armor making them bloom red flowers as heads shatter go on to the strife and trample the weak bodies into mud that stains the white marble brick beneath the beautiful sounds of chopping flesh and cutting bones go toward the strife fear hate crying out mountains of bodies burning all around you entrails and limbs of the defeated and the sacrificed go to the strife
**Furiae Archer:** I... I want...
**Philoc:** Child of strife, do you wish you go to battle?
**Furiae Archer:** No! I don't want to fight!
**Philoc:** Hmph. And I believed that you had become a citizen of Okhema. I knew it was wrong to place any hope in you.
**Furiae Archer:** Captain of Okhema...
**Philoc:** The moment of reckoning has come for the holy city. Sabany, Adian, Alflanke... The vassal states of Okhema have been reduced to scorched earth one by one. Now, the Lance of Fury points its tip at the city gates.
**Philoc:** A being born for war refuses to fight to protect this city. Could it be that you can't bear to fight with your past relatives? Perhaps you don't dare face the might of your creator? Or maybe there is someone in this city that you cannot leave?
**Furiae Archer:** ...
**Philoc:** Whatever the reason, I must tell you: That man that you cannot bear to part with is about to step onto the battlefield.
**Furiae Archer:** What?
**Philoc:** The military orders have been released. As a young man that is fit to fight, he has a duty to protect the sacred city, even if it costs his life. This is his fate, and he should be proud of it.
**Memory's Chorus:** Listen! Hear the sound of wailing from the front of the city gate: Wives call for their husbands and their elderly mothers, while others scream for their children. Just hear how they suffer.
**Memory's Chorus:** And you, you bow before the throne of Kephale with your stone face. Your emotionless expression has almost a smile in its place.
**Furiae Archer:** Go to the battlefield... If I am with Paris... Maybe...
*(From the depths of the memory arises a sorrow lament...)*
**Mem:** This memory tastes weird... Hm? What is Paris doing there?
*(Approach the marked location)*
**Paris:** Oh Mnestia, please grant me a romantic miracle!
> *(Trailblazer)* What's wrong?
> *(Trailblazer)* Do you need my help again?
**Paris:** Ah, my savior! It has been far too long! You always appear when I need you most. I cannot help but feel that fate is always watching out for me!
**Paris:** I'm sure you are aware that Okhema is in grave danger. As the forces of Castrum Kremnos have made it to the city gate, now we have to mobilize the guards of the city garrison to battle.
**Paris:** The enemy forces are attacking with the Revelation of Nikador. I fear any soldier that face them will meet a grim fate... But I don't want to die!
> *(Trailblazer)* This is your duty.
> *(Trailblazer)* You are truly useless.
> *(Trailblazer)* You want to desert your duty again?
**Paris:** My savior, it is not that I do not love this city that raised me. It's just that I have a dream that I have not yet completed... I have still not seen Arcadia. I don't want to enter the River of Souls without ever having laid eyes on it!
**Paris:** So, let them say what they will about me. Traitor, deserter, coward... My duty, my responsibility, and my mission, I will happily abandon them all, but I must reach that place.
**Paris:** I have already prepared my bags for my escape. But, before I go, there is someone that I hope would accompany me. You know who that is, right?
> *(Trailblazer)* The Titankin, right?
> *(Trailblazer)* Aren't you two moving a bit fast?
>> **Paris:** Hah, we have spent many days and nights together now. Anyone with sense can see how I feel for her.
> *(Trailblazer)* Me? But wouldn't that be wrong?
>> **Paris:** Haha, my savior, this is not the time to be joking with me.
>
> **Paris:** I want to leave with her. Even if we die on our path, we can fly into the hands of Thanatos together on the wings of romance. We can become butterflies and fly to that place together.
> **Paris:** Savior, this love of mine has always relied on your assistance. In my heart, you are like an embodiment of Mnestia herself.
> **Paris:** Now, I will confess my love and wishes to her. Please bear witness and give me your blessings and strength... This is the last favor I will ask of you.
> *(Trailblazer)* You have my blessings. Go for it!
> *(Trailblazer)* Nothing is more important than your happiness.
> *(Trailblazer)* I'll be here with my popcorn.
>
> **Paris:** Thank you, savior. I will go now. I pray that you can bring me good fortune once more.

*(...This thread can still glow faintly in the darkness. Even if it is broken and withered, people can still appreciate its lingering beauty... ### Part 305 — `databank/missions/adventure/v30.md`

**Context:** Adventure Missions — Version 3.0 (Heroic Saga of Flame-Chase) > I Once Was In Arcadia > Dialogue > Speak with the Furiae Archer


**Oenone:** Captain? Captain!?
**Paris:** Elder, he... He has been taken by the Hand of Shadow.
**Oenone:** Was... Was this our fault? Is this only happening because we allowed the Titankin of strife to enter the city!?
> *(Trailblazer)* Calm down...
> *(Trailblazer)* This is not your fault...
> *(Trailblazer)* Yes.
**Paris:** No, no, that's not true! This is all fate's fault!
**Paris:** It is fate that caused us to show kindness and mercy to the wrong person! It was fate that brought this dire ending on the captain!
**Oenone:** Ah, but how are we supposed to cast judgment on fate... The Titan that presides over judgment also sits atop its throne.
**Paris:** Elder Oenone, I am sorry... I cannot take part in this judgment. I must go. Someone is waiting for me outside the city.
**Oenone:** You... You are going to desert us? You are going to flee with that Titankin that is attacking our city as we speak?
**Oenone:** Paris. You must not allow yourself to be bewitched by this bringer of calamity any longer! I have already allowed you to make one mistake. I cannot allow you to continue!
**Paris:** But...
**Oenone:** If you side with her, you will forever bear her sins. You will become a villain, hated by the people of Okhema for eternity!
**Paris:** But...
**Oenone:** Further... Furthermore, the garrison guards will all place the death of Philoc on both of your heads! They may even bring you back to Okhema and see that you receive the most brutal of judgment!
**Paris:** But... But if I don't have her, who else will help me escape my terrifying duties and responsibilities? Who will believe my stories and go in search of Arcadia with me?
**Oenone:** ...Me.
**Oenone:** Paris, my hero. I am here for you. Why would you give the golden thread to a vile, evil creature like that and not return it to its original owner?
**Oenone:** With my influence, you can be spared from the battlefield and kept far away from the nightmares of strife. Let us escape to Dawncloud and spend our days in peace under the protection of Kephale.
**Oenone:** Then, when the black tide falls and strife has been defeated, we can ride in our grand carriage as nobles and go in search of our city of romance...
**Paris:** Arca... dia.
**Oenone:** That's right, Arcadia. With the golden thread as my witness, it is a land known to the people of my hometown. I can help you find it...
> *(Trailblazer)* What are you playing at?
>> **Oenone:** I'm just giving the person I love another option. Is there something wrong with that?
> *(Trailblazer)* A home wrecker knows when to strike.
>> **Oenone:** How dare you slander me like that? ...I am the original owner of the golden thread. Am I not far more suitable to accompany him than that monster?
> *(Trailblazer)* At least go and look for the statue lady!
>> **Oenone:** You're saying that Paris should run out onto the battlefield and risk his life among those insane Titankin to look for her?
>
> **Paris:** Savior, I... I think Oenone is right.
> **Paris:** I... I cannot take this risk. What if she really betrayed me... I, a normal man... am I not as frail as an ant before the Titankin? I cannot die. I cannot die...
> **Paris:** Exactly! Philoc would not lie to me. That girl... The Furiae Archer's battle instincts must have been awakened by her kin. She must have chosen to return to the forces of Castrum Kremnos... She has betrayed me...
> **Paris:** To fall in love with a woman from a rival city and abandon everything as I flee. This is the kind of romance I read in my stories, and that's how I became obsessed...
> **Oenone:** Paris. Make a decision quickly... The guards are coming. I cannot help clear the name of a soldier who attempts desertion.
> **Paris:** Ah! I cannot bear the blame of betrayal! Oenone. My Lady! Come and aid me!
> **Oenone:** Fear not, Paris. I will keep you away from that bringer of catastrophe and help you find the land of your dreams. Forget that liar, okay?
> **Paris:** Thank you, Oenone. Thank you for saving me...
>
> Surrounded by the sounds of the fighting outside the city, the two embrace as if wrapped in an unseeable cocoon. They stand beside the corpse of the captain, his eyes open past the precipice of death.
> The smiles on their faces are forced as fear devours any joy in their eyes. Though their faces are beautiful, there is something distorted about this image.
**Mem:** Partner... The memory here is starting to fade. The statue lady doesn't know what happens after this, so we cannot investigate any further...
However, what this does mean... Is that everything that happened here... she knew it was coming.
*(Receding Memory)*
*(On the outskirts of Okhema, the soldiers wander in doomed madness...)*
**Memory's Chorus:** Listen, listen now!
**Memory's Chorus:** Listen, quick, listen. There is a familiar voice in your soul.
**Furiae Archer:** It is strife... Strife has come. The golden blood you were gifted begins to stew, and the fated desire boils up within you...
**Memory's Chorus:** Will you respond to the call of your soul and throw your body into the strife? Or will you hold on to this golden thread and escape into the distance ahead?
**Furiae Archer:** I don't know. Both of these paths are my fate... One is my Strife, and the other is his Romance...
**Memory's Chorus:** But the roar of your instincts deafens you on the battlefield, so you still opt to fire the Arrow of Strife that you wield...
**Memory's Chorus:** Your first prey is your very own kin. On the Kremnos army's frontline, he gives you a salute, yet you shoot through his heart.
**Memory's Chorus:** Your second prey is your own family. He climbs the walls under the cover of night to feast on the dead, so you take your aim and shatter his head.
**Memory's Chorus:** Number three, number four, number five... You ceaselessly hurt your own kin without stopping for rest. Yet your bow never aimed at any citizen of Okhema.
**Furiae Archer:** It is the thin golden thread that still guides my hand. It will not allow my arrowheads to pierce that fragile romance...
**Memory's Chorus:** You wait and wait, and help the Garrison of Okhema fight off a whole wave of assault. But the man you would travel with never comes.
**Memory's Chorus:** You wish to return to the city, but the garrison guards greet you as an enemy.
**Memory's Chorus:** As your body is covered in wounds dealt by both the army of the holy city and that of Kremnos, you search for the kin that missed his appointment in a battlefield bereft of kin...
**Furiae Archer:** Pa... ris...
**Paris:** That girl... The Furiae Archer's battle instincts must have been awakened by her kin. She must have chosen to return to the forces of Castrum Kremnos... She has betrayed me...
**Memory's Chorus:** That's right. You are a traitor. You betrayed Castrum Kremnos and your family. Then you set to abandon the holy city that saved you. You are exactly the traitor they made you.
**Oenone:** Fear not, Paris. I will protect you. I will keep you away from that bringer of catastrophe and help you find the land of your dreams.
**Memory's Chorus:** That's right. You bring calamity, and you've caused Okhema to fall. The city was kind to you, but you brought strife on them all.
**Paris:** Let me cut this golden thread on my arm. I cannot have the traitor following us...
**Memory's Chorus:** Now, the thread is cut. Your hands will never again hold silver trinkets, kitchenware, or tapestries. All that remains are arrows and weaponry.
**Furiae Archer:** Now, there are no more choices open for me. Only one path of fate remains...
**Go to the Strife:** Go to the Strife
**Furiae Archer:** From that moment on, I've been into a long dream.
**Furiae Archer:** In my dream, I am a malnourished, starving lion. I can not think about anything but hunting for food.
**Furiae Archer:** So, I hunt, devour, and kill endlessly. I feel the biological sensation of hunger for the first time, so I swallow silver jewelry, cooking stoves, unfinished tapestries...
**Furiae Archer:** ...Until I see the ring with the broken thread around it once more, and I come to my senses in the wastelands under the black tide.
**Furiae Archer:** The man wearing the ring lies on the ground as my arrows guide the hand of Thanatos to claim his soul. The woman who travelled with him abandoned him. Their extravagant carriage, decked with flowers, sped away from his corpse without ever turning back.
**Furiae Archer:** To this day, I still do not understand what this "romance" is that he spoke of.
**Memory's Chorus:** The divine works in mysterious ways, and they care not for what a mortal man prays.
**Memory's Chorus:** An expectation is impossible for one to make real. When we know not where we're going, our path is revealed.
**Memory's Chorus:** This is how it ends.
*(Teleported to "Bloodbathed Battlefront" Castrum Kremnos)*
*(Having witnessed the Furiae Archer's memories, how would you describe this story? A tragedy? A misunderstanding? A lie? And the emotion in her voice when she pronounces those two names... Is it love? Hatred? Or simply confusion? Having explored this story to its end, you still cannot find an answer for yourself. If the flowers on the body that he so loves were to wither, he would sprout wings and fly away. It does not matter what sweet words he'd said before, or how deep a promise he had made.)*
*(Talk to Kremnos' Furiae Archer)*
As the lingering echoes of the memory fade, you return to the smoke-filled streets of Castrum Kremnos.
You've never found Arcadia, and Paris will never return. Yet the nameless Titankin still lies before you, murmuring.
**Mem:** This story... It ended differently to how I imagined...
**Furiae Archer:** Pa... ris...
As the Titankin's speech grows weaker, you bring your ear closer, hoping to hear her final words...
**Furiae Archer:** Arcadia... Arcadia...
**Furiae Archer:** Where... Could it be..
**Furiae Archer:** ...
The Titankin of Strife utters nothing more. She falls completely still, as if she had become a real statue.
**Mem:** Partner... her memory... It's disappeared...
Having lost her life, the Titankin's body is no different from a pile of rubble. You know that before long, the blood-stained winds of Castrum Kremnos will whip up the surrounding gravel and reduce it to dust, carrying it far into the distance.
Perhaps the land that this dust will be carried to is the true Arcadia? Does Cerces (Reason) know that place? Will Mnestia (Romance) await them there?
You do not know. All that you can hear is the echoing war drums of Nikador (Strife).
**Mem:** Statue lady... Your memory... We will never forget it...

**Acolyte:** It's getting more unsafe outside these days. The sounds outside the temple have been waning, and our offerings dwindle by the day.
**Acolyte:** Most of these ritual herbs have withered and died. Were they brought from within the black tide?
**Attendant:** Yes, Your Highness. It is said that we lost many a good man trying to flee the monsters spawned of the black tide — we couldn't even bring their bodies back home.
**Attendant:** But what other solutions do they have? All they have are the hopes of the temple's meager rewards to feed their families.
**Acolyte:** The relatives of the dead... note them down, and double their remunerations. This is Oronyx's (Time Titan) blessing unto them. I will pay for the portion that exceeds the allotted compensation.
**Acolyte:** Also tell them, in the near future, the temple will have no need of tribute or herbs. We have little else to reward them with. If they wish to live, tell them to go to the holy city of Okhema.
**Attendant:** My lord, you mean...?
**Acolyte:** I've already said what has to be said. As for the things I didn't say, I'm sure you know how to explain them to the commoners.
**Attendant:** Understood. This is absolutely not the destruction preordained by prophecy. Some of us are simply unhappy with the poverty that comes with worshiping the Fate Titans, so we were deceived by Zagreus and fled to Okhema.
**Acolyte:** Very good. Send the herbs to the Holy Maiden. The ritual to Oronyx is about to start.
**Attendant:** Before I leave, forgive me for asking — can we truly not escape this fate? We can persuade those stubborn priests to plead for aid from Okhema.
**Acolyte:** If Janusopolis is destined to fall, reinforcements will only cause more people to forsake their lives here.
**Attendant:** ...My lord, may the gods' gazes shine radiant on our glorious advance.
